# Run a program with a ceiling (PC watchdog). Agent runs also stop when progress stalls (issue 818).
# The program's output goes to this window (so "tmo.bat ... > log.txt 2>&1" captures it).
# Call it through tools\pc\tmo.bat <seconds> <exe> [args ...]: the bat hands the whole command line over in TMO_ARGS, so
# arguments that start with "-" or "--" reach the program untouched (PowerShell would read them as its own parameters).
param([switch]$LibraryOnly)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'

# runner_stall_tests.js runs this decision with fake clocks on Linux CI and probes real processes on Windows.
function Get-StopReason($Elapsed, $Idle, $Ceiling, $StallMinutes) {
  if ($Elapsed -ge $Ceiling) { return 'CEILING' }
  if ($Idle -ge ($StallMinutes * 60)) { return "STALLED after $StallMinutes min" }
  return ''
}
function Get-RunProgress($Repo, $Logs) {
  $files = @{}
  $dirs = New-Object 'System.Collections.Generic.Stack[string]'
  $dirs.Push($Repo)
  while ($dirs.Count) {
    foreach ($f in (Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $dirs.Pop() -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)) {
      # Reading git status may touch its index; Git housekeeping must not keep a silent agent alive.
      # Do not follow junctions outside this run's checkout.
      if ($f.Name -eq '.git' -or ($f.Attributes -band [IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint)) { continue }
      if ($f.PSIsContainer) { $dirs.Push($f.FullName) }
      else { $files[$f.FullName] = "$($f.LastWriteTimeUtc.Ticks):$($f.Length)" }
    }
  }
  $head = (& git.exe -C $Repo rev-parse --verify HEAD 2>$null) -join ''
  $bytes = @{}
  foreach ($path in $Logs) {
    $f = Get-Item -LiteralPath $path -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    $bytes[$path] = if ($f) { $f.Length } else { 0 }
  }
  return @{ Files = $files; Head = $head; Bytes = $bytes }
}
function Test-RunProgress($Before, $After) {
  if ($After.Head -and $After.Head -ne $Before.Head) { return $true }
  if ($After.Files.Count -ne $Before.Files.Count) { return $true }
  foreach ($key in $After.Files.Keys) {
    if (-not $Before.Files.ContainsKey($key) -or $Before.Files[$key] -ne $After.Files[$key]) { return $true }
  }
  foreach ($key in $After.Bytes.Keys) {
    if ($After.Bytes[$key] -gt $Before.Bytes[$key]) { return $true }
  }
  return $false
}
function Save-RunResume($Repo, $Out, $Logs, $Reason, $Base) {
  $utf8 = New-Object Text.UTF8Encoding($false)
  $changed = @(& git.exe --no-optional-locks -C $Repo -c core.quotepath=off diff --name-only $Base -- 2>$null)
  $changed += @(& git.exe --no-optional-locks -C $Repo -c core.quotepath=off ls-files --others --exclude-standard 2>$null)
  $changed = @($changed | Sort-Object -Unique)
  $note = "## Runner resume note (issue 818)`nStop reason: $Reason`n`nChanged files (from run base $Base):`n"
  if ($changed.Count) { $note += ($changed | ForEach-Object { "- $_" }) -join "`n" }
  else { $note += '(none)' }
  foreach ($path in $Logs) {
    $note += "`n`nLast log lines ($([IO.Path]::GetFileName($path))):`n"
    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $path) {
      $tail = @(Get-Content -LiteralPath $path -Tail 12 -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | ForEach-Object {
        $line = [string]$_; if ($line.Length -gt 400) { $line = $line.Substring(0, 400) + ' [cut]' }; $line
      })
      $note += $tail -join "`n"
    } else { $note += '(no output)' }
  }
  $note += "`n## End runner resume note`n"
  [IO.File]::WriteAllText((Join-Path $Out 'resume.txt'), $note, $utf8)
  [IO.File]::WriteAllText((Join-Path $Out 'stop_reason.txt'), $Reason, $utf8)
}
# taskkill /T uses process enumeration that can be denied in the Codex sandbox, even for our own children.
# Toolhelp needs no WMI access. Keep process handles, not just PIDs, so a reused PID can never target another job.
function Initialize-RunTree {
  if ('RunnerProcessSnapshot' -as [type]) { return }
  Add-Type -TypeDefinition @'
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
public static class RunnerProcessSnapshot {
  [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential, CharSet = CharSet.Unicode)]
  struct Entry {
    public uint size, usage, id; public UIntPtr heap; public uint module, threads, parent;
    public int priority; public uint flags;
    [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.ByValTStr, SizeConst = 260)] public string exe;
  }
  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern IntPtr CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(uint flags, uint id);
  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", CharSet = CharSet.Unicode, SetLastError = true)] static extern bool Process32FirstW(IntPtr h, ref Entry e);
  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", CharSet = CharSet.Unicode, SetLastError = true)] static extern bool Process32NextW(IntPtr h, ref Entry e);
  [DllImport("kernel32.dll")] static extern bool CloseHandle(IntPtr h);
  public static Dictionary<int, int> Parents() {
    IntPtr h = CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(2, 0);
    if (h == new IntPtr(-1)) throw new Win32Exception();
    try {
      var result = new Dictionary<int, int>();
      var e = new Entry(); e.size = (uint)Marshal.SizeOf(typeof(Entry));
      if (!Process32FirstW(h, ref e)) throw new Win32Exception();
      do { result[(int)e.id] = (int)e.parent; } while (Process32NextW(h, ref e));
      if (Marshal.GetLastWin32Error() != 18) throw new Win32Exception();
      return result;
    } finally { CloseHandle(h); }
  }
}
'@
}
function Update-RunTree($Tree) {
  $parents = [RunnerProcessSnapshot]::Parents()
  do {
    $added = $false
    foreach ($childId in $parents.Keys) {
      $parentId = $parents[$childId]
      if ($Tree.ContainsKey($childId) -or -not $Tree.ContainsKey($parentId)) { continue }
      $parent = $Tree[$parentId]
      # A retained handle keeps the original process identity after exit. StartTime also rejects reused parent PIDs.
      try {
        $child = [Diagnostics.Process]::GetProcessById($childId)
        $null = $child.Handle
        if ($child.StartTime -lt $parent.StartTime) { $child.Dispose(); continue }
        if ($parent.HasExited -and $child.StartTime -gt $parent.ExitTime) { $child.Dispose(); continue }
        $Tree[$childId] = $child; $added = $true
      } catch [ArgumentException] { } # The child already exited between snapshot and open.
    }
  } while ($added)
}
function Write-KillLog($Log, $Line) {
  Write-Output "run_timeout: $Line"
  try { "$(Get-Date -Format s) $Line" | Out-File -LiteralPath $Log -Append -Encoding utf8 }
  catch { Write-Output "run_timeout: could not append kill log: $($_.Exception.Message)" }
}
function Stop-RunTree($Process, $Tree, $Log) {
  Update-RunTree $Tree
  $ea = $ErrorActionPreference
  $killer = 'taskkill.exe'
  if ($env:PC_RUNNER_TEST -eq '1' -and $env:PC_TEST_TASKKILL) { $killer = $env:PC_TEST_TASKKILL }
  try {
    $ErrorActionPreference = 'Continue'
    $reply = @(& $killer /PID $Process.Id /T /F 2>&1)
    $code = $LASTEXITCODE
  } finally { $ErrorActionPreference = $ea }
  Write-KillLog $Log "taskkill PID $($Process.Id) exit ${code}: $($reply -join ' ')"
  $deadline = [DateTime]::UtcNow.AddSeconds(15)
  do {
    # Discover children before stopping their parents, and repeat in case one spawned during taskkill.
    Update-RunTree $Tree
    $live = @($Tree.Values | Where-Object { -not $_.HasExited } | Sort-Object StartTime -Descending)
    foreach ($child in $live) {
      try { $child.Kill() }
      catch { if (-not $child.HasExited) { Write-KillLog $Log "handle kill PID $($child.Id) failed: $($_.Exception.Message)" } }
    }
    foreach ($child in $live) { $null = $child.WaitForExit(100) }
    Update-RunTree $Tree
    $live = @($Tree.Values | Where-Object { -not $_.HasExited })
    if (-not $live.Count) {
      Write-KillLog $Log "kill verified: all $($Tree.Count) processes gone (PIDs $((@($Tree.Keys) | Sort-Object) -join ', '))"
      return
    }
  } while ([DateTime]::UtcNow -lt $deadline)
  $failure = "KILL FAILED: surviving PIDs $(($live | ForEach-Object { $_.Id }) -join ', '); checkout kept"
  Write-KillLog $Log $failure
  throw $failure
}
if ($LibraryOnly) { return }
$raw = $env:TMO_ARGS
if (-not $raw) { Write-Output 'usage: tmo.bat <seconds> <exe> [args ...]'; exit 125 }
$m = [regex]::Match($raw, '^\s*(\d+)\s+("([^"]+)"|(\S+))\s*(.*)$')
if (-not $m.Success) { Write-Output "run_timeout: cannot read the command line: $raw"; exit 125 }
$Seconds = [int]$m.Groups[1].Value
$Exe = if ($m.Groups[3].Success) { $m.Groups[3].Value } else { $m.Groups[4].Value }
$argLine = $m.Groups[5].Value
$root = Join-Path $env:USERPROFILE 'Downloads\voidsworn_test'

if (-not (Test-Path $logDir)) { $logDir = $PSScriptRoot }
$log = Join-Path $logDir 'timeouts.log'
if ($env:PC_RUNNER_TEST -eq '1' -and $env:PC_TEST_TIMEOUT_LOG) { $log = $env:PC_TEST_TIMEOUT_LOG }
$watch = $env:TMO_REPO -and $env:TMO_OUT -and $env:TMO_LOG
$watchRepo = $env:TMO_REPO; $watchOut = $env:TMO_OUT
$stall = 30.0
if ($watch -and $env:STALL_MIN) { $stall = [double]::Parse($env:STALL_MIN, [Globalization.CultureInfo]::InvariantCulture) }
if ($Seconds -le 0 -or $stall -le 0 -or [double]::IsNaN($stall) -or [double]::IsInfinity($stall)) { throw 'ceiling and STALL_MIN must be positive' }
$logs = @($env:TMO_LOG, $env:TMO_ERR_LOG) | Where-Object { $_ }
if ($watch) {
  $before = Get-RunProgress $watchRepo $logs
  $base = $before.Head
  $bp = Join-Path $watchOut 'base_commit.txt'
  if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $bp) { $base = [IO.File]::ReadAllText($bp).Trim() }
}
# Nov 1: a test job ran all through Ryan's first Godot playtest, slowing the PC and overwriting the game's log.
# Wait while Ryan is playing: the game is Voidsworn.exe or a windowed Godot started with no job arguments
# (play_godot.bat passes none; every job passes --headless, --script, --bot or similar). Waiting is not timed.
function Test-RyanPlaying {
  try { $ps = Get-CimInstance Win32_Process -Filter "Name LIKE 'Voidsworn%.exe' OR Name LIKE 'Godot%.exe'" } catch { return $false }
  foreach ($q in $ps) {
    if ($q.Name -like '*_console.exe') { continue }
    $cl = [string]$q.CommandLine
    $rest = [regex]::Replace($cl, '^\s*("[^"]*"|\S+)', '')
    $rest = [regex]::Replace($rest, '--path\s+("[^"]*"|\S+)', '')
    if ($rest -notmatch '--?\w') { return $true }
  }
  return $false
}
$w0 = Get-Date; $said = $false
# Codex jobs are light (one model session, small node scripts) and do not touch the game's log: they run during play.
$noWait = ($Exe -match 'codex(\.cmd|\.exe)?$') -or ($env:PC_RUNNER_TEST -eq '1')
while ((-not $noWait) -and (Test-RyanPlaying) -and ((Get-Date) - $w0).TotalHours -lt 8) {
  if (-not $said) { Write-Output "run_timeout: the game is open, waiting until it closes"; "$(Get-Date -Format s) WAIT game open: $Exe" | Out-File $log -Append -Encoding utf8; $said = $true }
  Start-Sleep -Seconds 20
}
if ($said) { "$(Get-Date -Format s) RESUME after $([int]((Get-Date) - $w0).TotalMinutes) min: $Exe" | Out-File $log -Append -Encoding utf8 }
# R293: acquire only around the GPU command, never the whole model session.
# A pending solo claim drains all shared slots and blocks new workers.
$gpuHelper = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'gpu_slots.js'
$gpuNode = 'node.exe'
$bundledNode = Join-Path $root 'node\node.exe'
if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $bundledNode) { $gpuNode = $bundledNode }
$gpuRoot = $root
if ($env:PC_RUNNER_TEST -eq '1' -and $env:PC_TEST_GPU_ROOT) { $gpuRoot = $env:PC_TEST_GPU_ROOT }
$gpuToken = ''
try {
$classify = @{ exe = $Exe; args = $argLine; cwd = (Get-Location).Path; requested = [string]$env:PC_GPU_MODE; weight = [string]$env:PC_GPU_WEIGHT } | ConvertTo-Json -Compress
$gpuClass = ($classify | & $gpuNode $gpuHelper classify) -join ''
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'GPU command classification failed' }
$gpuClass = $gpuClass | ConvertFrom-Json
$gpuMode = $gpuClass.mode
if ($gpuMode) {
  Write-Output "GPU: waiting for $gpuMode claim, weight $($gpuClass.weight) (issue $env:PC_GPU_ISSUE)"
  # Admission has its own clock in gpu_slots.js (PC_GPU_WAIT_SECONDS, default 300).
  # Read the helper's diagnostic even under Windows PowerShell's native stderr handling.
  $ea = $ErrorActionPreference
  try {
    $ErrorActionPreference = 'Continue'
    & $gpuNode $gpuHelper acquire $gpuRoot ([string]$env:PC_GPU_ISSUE + ':gpu') $PID $gpuMode $gpuClass.weight 2>&1 | ForEach-Object {
      $line = [string]$_
      if ($line -eq 'WAIT') { Write-Host "GPU: still waiting for $gpuMode claim" }
      elseif ($line -cmatch '\A[0-9a-f]{8}(?:-[0-9a-f]{4}){3}-[0-9a-f]{12}\z') { $gpuToken = $line }
      else { Write-Output $line }
    }
    $admissionExit = $LASTEXITCODE
  } finally { $ErrorActionPreference = $ea }
  if ($admissionExit -ne 0 -or -not $gpuToken) {
    Write-Output 'GPU: admission failed; command was not started'
    exit 126
  }
  Write-Output "GPU: acquired $gpuMode claim $gpuToken"
}
# A forced claim applies to this command only, never a nested watchdog.
$env:PC_GPU_MODE = ''
$env:PC_GPU_WEIGHT = ''
Initialize-RunTree
$timer = [Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
$lastProgress = 0.0
$scanEvery = 60.0
if ($env:PC_RUNNER_TEST -eq '1' -and $env:PC_TEST_SCAN_S) { $scanEvery = [double]$env:PC_TEST_SCAN_S }
$nextScan = $scanEvery
# Child commands may use tmo.bat too. Their own timeout must never overwrite this agent's resume note.
$env:TMO_REPO = ''; $env:TMO_OUT = ''; $env:TMO_LOG = ''; $env:TMO_ERR_LOG = ''
try {
  if ($argLine -ne '') { $p = Start-Process -FilePath $Exe -ArgumentList $argLine -NoNewWindow -PassThru }
  else { $p = Start-Process -FilePath $Exe -NoNewWindow -PassThru }
} catch {
  "$(Get-Date -Format s) START FAILED $Exe $argLine : $_" | Out-File $log -Append -Encoding utf8
  Write-Output "run_timeout: could not start $Exe : $_"
  exit 125
}
$null = $p.Handle   # keeps the exit code readable after the process ends
$tree = @{ }; $tree[$p.Id] = $p
$completed = $false
try {
$poll = 5000
if ($env:PC_RUNNER_TEST -eq '1' -and $env:PC_TEST_POLL_MS) { $poll = [int]$env:PC_TEST_POLL_MS }
while ($true) {
  Update-RunTree $tree
  $remaining = [Math]::Max(1, [Math]::Min($poll, ($Seconds - $timer.Elapsed.TotalSeconds) * 1000))
  if ($p.WaitForExit([int]$remaining)) { $p.WaitForExit(); $completed = $true; exit $p.ExitCode }
  if ($watch) {
    # Scan the checkout once a minute, not every poll: a full walk of a big checkout every 5 s would load Ryan's PC for hours,
    # and a 30 minute stall window does not need finer steps (Board review, Nov 15).
    if ($timer.Elapsed.TotalSeconds -ge $nextScan) {
      $after = Get-RunProgress $watchRepo $logs
      if (Test-RunProgress $before $after) { $lastProgress = $timer.Elapsed.TotalSeconds }
      $before = $after
      $nextScan = $timer.Elapsed.TotalSeconds + $scanEvery
    }
    $reason = Get-StopReason $timer.Elapsed.TotalSeconds ($timer.Elapsed.TotalSeconds - $lastProgress) $Seconds $stall
  } else {
    $reason = if ($timer.Elapsed.TotalSeconds -ge $Seconds) { 'CEILING' } else { '' }
  }
  if ($p.HasExited) { $p.WaitForExit(); $completed = $true; exit $p.ExitCode }
  if ($reason) { break }
}
$secs = [int]$timer.Elapsed.TotalSeconds
Stop-RunTree $p $tree $log
if ($watch) { Save-RunResume $watchRepo $watchOut $logs $reason $base }
"$(Get-Date -Format s) TIMEOUT $reason after $secs s (ceiling $Seconds s), verified dead tree of PID $($p.Id): $Exe $argLine" | Out-File $log -Append -Encoding utf8
Write-Output "run_timeout: TIMEOUT $reason after $secs s, killed $Exe (PID $($p.Id)) and its children"
exit 124
} finally {
  # Reap on an unexpected watchdog error too. A successful queue launcher may deliberately start background jobs.
  if (-not $completed) {
    Update-RunTree $tree
    if (@($tree.Values | Where-Object { -not $_.HasExited }).Count) { Stop-RunTree $p $tree $log }
  }
  foreach ($child in $tree.Values) { $child.Dispose() }
}
} finally {
  if ($gpuToken) {
    & $gpuNode $gpuHelper release $gpuRoot $gpuToken
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { Write-Output "GPU: release failed; dead owner PID $PID will be reclaimed" }
  }
}

@echo off
rem Run a program with a time limit: tmo.bat <seconds> <exe> [args ...]
rem Kills the whole process tree if it overruns (exit code 124, logged to Downloads\voidsworn_test\queue\logs\timeouts.log);
rem otherwise returns the program's own exit code. Its output comes out here, so "tmo.bat ... > log.txt 2>&1" captures it.
rem For agent runs set TMO_REPO, TMO_OUT and TMO_LOG. The seconds argument is then the hard ceiling (runners default
rem to 21600), and STALL_MIN (default 30) stops a run only after no file, commit or log progress. TMO_ERR_LOG is optional.
rem A stopped agent writes resume.txt and stop_reason.txt in TMO_OUT. Clear these TMO variables before runner checks.
rem R293: shared capacity is in Downloads\voidsworn_test\gpu_slots.json, e.g. {"sharedSlots":4}, default 2.
rem Godot GPU commands weigh 1, Blender commands weigh 2; PC_GPU_WEIGHT overrides the weight for this command.
rem For a CPU-only Blender command set PC_GPU_MODE=none. Godot still classifies its own GPU work.
rem Timing scripts take solo and wait for all slots, whatever the shared capacity.
rem For other GPU executables set PC_GPU_MODE=shared, or solo for timing. Waiting does not spend the command's limit.
setlocal
set "TMO_ARGS=%*"
powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File "%~dp0run_timeout.ps1"
exit /b %ERRORLEVEL%


 succeeded in 44ms:

3.1 **The product is the Godot 4 game on Ryan's Windows PC only; the browser rules (data.js, core.js, battle.js and the
campaign files) are a live reference copy: every rules change goes into both until Ryan approves their retirement.** The browser
game and pages ship nothing. Parity runs in full on every push, never skipped and never cut (no skip rule), made faster by
running battles in parallel workers (issue 978), and a Godot-only safety net runs beside it (issue 979, then saved Godot
results with a changed-battles review on rules PRs, piloted for whether its review cost is worth it). Ten rules PRs is a
progress checkpoint, not approval to retire parity. The browser rules stay until the replacement data home, a Godot balance
simulator, the ported tests and the PC tools work and are verified; retirement is then a separate recommendation to Ryan.
Why: "Godot seems way better. It's very noticeable now." (R32); PC-only (Oct 24); "Let's go with Path D ... Don't implement
the skip rule or reduce coverage ... Keep the browser rules until the replacement data, balance simulator, relevant tests and
tools are working and verified, and bring me a separate retirement recommendation then." (R312). The old "frozen" wording was
wrong: every new rule since went into both copies (round 2 review, Nov 17). Set: Ryan. Ids: R32, R33a, agents-02, R312.

3.2 **Heavy work runs on Ryan's PC (RTX 5080) through the queue; the cloud sandbox is for editing.** Why: the sandbox has about
8 GB and no GPU and runs out of memory on full meshes. Set: Ryan, Oct 21. Id: agents-23.

3.3 **Nothing on Ryan's PC is deleted except a job's own leftovers.** A job may remove the temp copies and scratch folders it created, and old kept workspace copies that Git shows hold only rebuildable files, once its work is pushed. Everything else stays until Ryan says yes. Keep every job's PC folder lean, and the Board lists the PC folder sizes at each disk check.
Why: the PC disk filled on Nov 11 (C: 0.0 GB free, 550 GB in voidsworn_test, mostly old run copies), the queue
watcher stopped and every PC job waited until Ryan freed space himself (LOOP.md, Nov 12 02:15 and 02:20 UTC). On Nov 18 the drive fell under the 60 GB job floor from about 45 kept workspace copies and 390 GB of job scratch in the temp folder; Ryan chose (Nov 18 20:46 UTC) to let jobs clean up after themselves (R326).
Set: Ryan, Nov 18 (R326); lean folders and the disk check Board, Nov 11. Ids: ships-10, loop-17.

Before setting aside an earlier checkout, the runner reuses it only when its working tree is clean and all local history
(including reflogs) is in the freshly fetched mirror. It allows ignored Godot `.godot` and `.import` caches, Python
`__pycache__` and `.pyc` caches, and ignored files under `godot/assets/ships/` whose size and SHA-256 match the same path
in the shared build (issue 1107). It fetches and switches to the new branch with those files in place. A collision or an
uncertain check keeps the earlier checkout beside repo. More than two `repo-kept-*` copies triggers a warning naming each
copy and its size; older copies are removed only as the next paragraph allows. Why: repeated full copies filled C:, and deleting a supposedly clean checkout could lose
unique ignored work (issue 860, run 2, Board Nov 16).
A checkout that changes while the runner starts is never reset; the job stops instead.

Parity removes its own default temp folder when it finishes, pass or fail. After a checked push the runner removes
the scratch folder it recorded for that run. It keeps the newest `repo-kept-*` copy in the board folder and removes
older ones only when the same rebuildable-file and Git history checks pass. It logs each decision. A pause removes nothing (3.11).

3.4 **GPU work is sized to the job and to what the RTX 5080 can hold, not to a fixed job count; frame-time measurements run
solo. Lanes C and D stay code only.** The Board decides per job how many share the card: light captures and probes can run side by
side, heavy bakes and Blender renders take more of it, and nothing is started that would run the card out of its 16 GB of
graphics memory. The launchers take and release claims themselves through tools/pc/gpu_slots.js (shared capacity from
Downloads\voidsworn_test\gpu_slots.json, {"sharedSlots":2} by default, raised by the Board; captures weigh 1, Blender and heavy
bakes 2, with PC_GPU_WEIGHT for the job's chosen weight), or a solo claim that waits for every slot
to empty and blocks new GPU work while it measures. Dead process claims are reclaimed. `gpu_go.txt` still wins: no new GPU
work starts unless its first line is `GPU cleared` (optionally followed by `: reason`); the gaming pause in 3.11 is
unchanged. Why: "that one GPU job at a time is an outdated rule, we should be flexible based on the task and what my
graphicsa card can actually handle as a 5080. we don't want to overwhelm it but we don't want to artificially restrict small
jobs and extend things out" (Ryan, Nov 16, R293). Solo measurements keep speed numbers honest. Replaces the two-job cap
(R276, Nov 15) and the one-job limit before it (R173b, effects-09); the gaming pause (R173) remains. Set: Ryan, Nov 16 (R293).

3.5 **Every PC queue job is wrapped in the tmo.bat watchdog, ends with exit /b, and carries no |, &, < or > in its
arguments.** Agent runs stop after STALL_MIN minutes without progress (default 30), or at a hard ceiling (default six
hours; CODEX_CAP and WORK_CAP override the ceiling in seconds). Progress is a changed file write time in the run repo,
a new commit, or new bytes in log.txt (also stderr for workhorse runs). Git housekeeping does not count. A stall or ceiling
stop writes its reason, changed files and last log lines to the out folder's resume.txt and the -partial commit message;
the next run reads that note. timeouts.log records STALLED after N min or CEILING. Short queue commands and runner checks
keep their explicit time limits. Why: hung Godot windows blocked the queue for about three hours (G-3), but the old two-hour
agent cap killed jobs that were still working (367 and 371). Ryan, Nov 15: "do we need to get rid of that random 2 hour rule
if it's timing out actual work?" He took up a stall check, a high backstop and a saved resume note (issue 818). Set: Board
(G-3), Ryan (issue 818). Ids: G-3, feel-06, skill-pb-05.

3.6 **The Board may double-click .bat files on the PC itself by computer use, a few clicks at a time, releasing the lock at
once, never while Ryan is playing; it never drives the PC by remote clicks to unstick a job.** Why: "can't you use computer

## How a run works

1. Read HANDOFF.md section 0 and this file. Set the lock and push it first.
2. Board check: open PRs, open issues and labels (gh api REST, never GraphQL). PC queue folders only when a PC job is running.
3. Review each new PR yourself, by diff, never by title, then sweep its impact (R129): every caller of each changed function, save and old-profile behaviour, the JS and Godot twins, every UI path that shows it, which tests actually ran in CI. Check claims against the done-when, look at the shots. Read the PR's Artoo comments on its current head commit and settle each (verify against live main; evidence, not rulings; R295). Merge if good; hold what Holds lists; file a follow-up for anything the sweep finds. Outside-check conditions still open at merge become issues (the PR 680 form) or a line in the PR's FINDINGS row, so none rides on memory (Muse audit 706 finding 10).
4. Release the next job only to a live seat, one at a time. Before releasing, check it is not already queued or running.
5. Anything for Ryan goes under "Waiting on Ryan" with a pick and plain trade-offs. In an unattended run use one PushNotification, never AskUserQuestion.
6. Tell Ryan only when something lands or needs him: two lines and one image.
7. Rewrite this file, set "Running: no", commit straight to main (this file and FINDINGS only, docs-only exception), push with timeout 90. Stop.
8. One send_later check only when a job or a limit reset lands well before the next look. Never chain them.

# Run a program with a ceiling and a progress watchdog (issues 818 and 1192).
# The program's output goes to this window (so "tmo.bat ... > log.txt 2>&1" captures it).
# Call it through tools\pc\tmo.bat <seconds> <exe> [args ...]: the bat hands the whole command line over in TMO_ARGS, so
# arguments that start with "-" or "--" reach the program untouched (PowerShell would read them as its own parameters).
param([switch]$LibraryOnly)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'

# runner_stall_tests.js runs this decision with fake clocks on Linux CI and probes real processes on Windows.
function Get-StopReason($Elapsed, $Idle, $Ceiling, $StallMinutes) {
  if ($Elapsed -ge $Ceiling) { return 'CEILING' }
  if ($Idle -ge ($StallMinutes * 60)) { return "STALLED after $StallMinutes min" }
  return ''
}
function Get-RunProgress($Repo, $Logs) {
  $files = @{}
  $dirs = New-Object 'System.Collections.Generic.Stack[string]'
  $dirs.Push($Repo)
  while ($dirs.Count) {
    foreach ($f in (Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $dirs.Pop() -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)) {
      # Reading git status may touch its index; Git housekeeping must not keep a silent agent alive.
      # Do not follow junctions outside this run's checkout.
      if ($f.Name -eq '.git' -or ($f.Attributes -band [IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint)) { continue }
      if ($f.PSIsContainer) { $dirs.Push($f.FullName) }
      else { $files[$f.FullName] = "$($f.LastWriteTimeUtc.Ticks):$($f.Length)" }
    }
  }
  $head = (& git.exe -C $Repo rev-parse --verify HEAD 2>$null) -join ''
  $bytes = @{}
  foreach ($path in $Logs) {
    $f = Get-Item -LiteralPath $path -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    $bytes[$path] = if ($f) { $f.Length } else { 0 }
  }
  return @{ Files = $files; Head = $head; Bytes = $bytes }
}
function Test-RunProgress($Before, $After) {
  if ($After.Head -and $After.Head -ne $Before.Head) { return $true }
  if ($After.Files.Count -ne $Before.Files.Count) { return $true }
  foreach ($key in $After.Files.Keys) {
    if (-not $Before.Files.ContainsKey($key) -or $Before.Files[$key] -ne $After.Files[$key]) { return $true }
  }
  foreach ($key in $After.Bytes.Keys) {
    if ($After.Bytes[$key] -gt $Before.Bytes[$key]) { return $true }
  }
  return $false
}
function Save-RunResume($Repo, $Out, $Logs, $Reason, $Base) {
  $utf8 = New-Object Text.UTF8Encoding($false)
  $changed = @(& git.exe --no-optional-locks -C $Repo -c core.quotepath=off diff --name-only $Base -- 2>$null)
  $changed += @(& git.exe --no-optional-locks -C $Repo -c core.quotepath=off ls-files --others --exclude-standard 2>$null)
  $changed = @($changed | Sort-Object -Unique)
  $note = "## Runner resume note (issue 818)`nStop reason: $Reason`n`nChanged files (from run base $Base):`n"
  if ($changed.Count) { $note += ($changed | ForEach-Object { "- $_" }) -join "`n" }
  else { $note += '(none)' }
  foreach ($path in $Logs) {
    $note += "`n`nLast log lines ($([IO.Path]::GetFileName($path))):`n"
    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $path) {
      $tail = @(Get-Content -LiteralPath $path -Tail 12 -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | ForEach-Object {
        $line = [string]$_; if ($line.Length -gt 400) { $line = $line.Substring(0, 400) + ' [cut]' }; $line
      })
      $note += $tail -join "`n"
    } else { $note += '(no output)' }
  }
  $note += "`n## End runner resume note`n"
  [IO.File]::WriteAllText((Join-Path $Out 'resume.txt'), $note, $utf8)
  [IO.File]::WriteAllText((Join-Path $Out 'stop_reason.txt'), $Reason, $utf8)
}
# taskkill /T uses process enumeration that can be denied in the Codex sandbox, even for our own children.
# Toolhelp needs no WMI access. Keep process handles, not just PIDs, so a reused PID can never target another job.
function Initialize-RunTree {
  if ('RunnerProcessSnapshot' -as [type]) { return }
  Add-Type -TypeDefinition @'
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
public static class RunnerProcessSnapshot {
  [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential, CharSet = CharSet.Unicode)]
  struct Entry {
    public uint size, usage, id; public UIntPtr heap; public uint module, threads, parent;
    public int priority; public uint flags;
    [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.ByValTStr, SizeConst = 260)] public string exe;
  }
  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern IntPtr CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(uint flags, uint id);
  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", CharSet = CharSet.Unicode, SetLastError = true)] static extern bool Process32FirstW(IntPtr h, ref Entry e);
  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", CharSet = CharSet.Unicode, SetLastError = true)] static extern bool Process32NextW(IntPtr h, ref Entry e);
  [DllImport("kernel32.dll")] static extern bool CloseHandle(IntPtr h);
  public static Dictionary<int, int> Parents() {
    IntPtr h = CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(2, 0);
    if (h == new IntPtr(-1)) throw new Win32Exception();
    try {
      var result = new Dictionary<int, int>();
      var e = new Entry(); e.size = (uint)Marshal.SizeOf(typeof(Entry));
      if (!Process32FirstW(h, ref e)) throw new Win32Exception();
      do { result[(int)e.id] = (int)e.parent; } while (Process32NextW(h, ref e));
      if (Marshal.GetLastWin32Error() != 18) throw new Win32Exception();
      return result;
    } finally { CloseHandle(h); }
  }
}
'@
}
function Update-RunTree($Tree) {
  $parents = [RunnerProcessSnapshot]::Parents()
  do {
    $added = $false
    foreach ($childId in $parents.Keys) {
      $parentId = $parents[$childId]
      if ($Tree.ContainsKey($childId) -or -not $Tree.ContainsKey($parentId)) { continue }
      $parent = $Tree[$parentId]
      # A retained handle keeps the original process identity after exit. StartTime also rejects reused parent PIDs.
      try {
        $child = [Diagnostics.Process]::GetProcessById($childId)
        $null = $child.Handle
        if ($child.StartTime -lt $parent.StartTime) { $child.Dispose(); continue }
        if ($parent.HasExited -and $child.StartTime -gt $parent.ExitTime) { $child.Dispose(); continue }
        $Tree[$childId] = $child; $added = $true
      } catch [ArgumentException] { } # The child already exited between snapshot and open.
    }
  } while ($added)
}
function Write-KillLog($Log, $Line) {
  Write-Output "run_timeout: $Line"
  for ($attempt = 1; ; $attempt++) {
    try { [IO.File]::AppendAllText($Log, "$(Get-Date -Format s) $Line`r`n"); return }
    catch [IO.IOException] {
      # Several watchdogs can report to the same queue log.
      if ($attempt -ge 10) { Write-Output "run_timeout: could not append kill log: $($_.Exception.Message)"; return }
      Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 25
    }
    catch { Write-Output "run_timeout: could not append kill log: $($_.Exception.Message)"; return }
  }
}
function Stop-RunTree($Process, $Tree, $Log) {
  Update-RunTree $Tree
  $ea = $ErrorActionPreference
  $killer = 'taskkill.exe'
  if ($env:PC_RUNNER_TEST -eq '1' -and $env:PC_TEST_TASKKILL) { $killer = $env:PC_TEST_TASKKILL }
  try {
    $ErrorActionPreference = 'Continue'
    $reply = @(& $killer /PID $Process.Id /T /F 2>&1)
    $code = $LASTEXITCODE
  } finally { $ErrorActionPreference = $ea }
  Write-KillLog $Log "taskkill PID $($Process.Id) exit ${code}: $($reply -join ' ')"
  $deadline = [DateTime]::UtcNow.AddSeconds(15)
  do {
    # Discover children before stopping their parents, and repeat in case one spawned during taskkill.
    Update-RunTree $Tree
    $live = @($Tree.Values | Where-Object { -not $_.HasExited } | Sort-Object StartTime -Descending)
    foreach ($child in $live) {
      try { $child.Kill() }
      catch { if (-not $child.HasExited) { Write-KillLog $Log "handle kill PID $($child.Id) failed: $($_.Exception.Message)" } }
    }
    foreach ($child in $live) { $null = $child.WaitForExit(100) }
    Update-RunTree $Tree
    $live = @($Tree.Values | Where-Object { -not $_.HasExited })
    if (-not $live.Count) {
      Write-KillLog $Log "kill verified: all $($Tree.Count) processes gone (PIDs $((@($Tree.Keys) | Sort-Object) -join ', '))"
      return
    }
  } while ([DateTime]::UtcNow -lt $deadline)
  $failure = "KILL FAILED: surviving PIDs $(($live | ForEach-Object { $_.Id }) -join ', '); checkout kept"
  Write-KillLog $Log $failure
  throw $failure
}
function Initialize-StepWatchdog {
  # Start suspended and assign the job before any child code can spawn a process. Windows keeps descendants in
  # the job even after their parent exits and accounts for CPU used by children that exit between polls.
  Add-Type -TypeDefinition @'
using System;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Text;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
using System.Threading;

public sealed class RunnerStep : IDisposable {
  [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]
  struct Startup {
    public int size;
    public IntPtr reserved, desktop, title;
    public int x, y, width, height, xChars, yChars, fill, flags;
    public short show, reservedSize;
    public IntPtr reservedBytes, input, output, error;
  }
  [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]
  struct ProcessInfo {
    public IntPtr process, thread;
    public int pid, tid;
  }
  [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]
  public struct Usage {
    public long UserTime, KernelTime, PeriodUserTime, PeriodKernelTime;
    public uint PageFaults, Total, Active, Terminated;
    public long CpuTime { get { return UserTime + KernelTime; } }
  }
  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern IntPtr CreateJobObject(IntPtr attrs, IntPtr name);
  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool SetInformationJobObject(IntPtr job, int kind, byte[] data, int size);
  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool QueryInformationJobObject(IntPtr job, int kind, out Usage data, int size, IntPtr written);
  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool AssignProcessToJobObject(IntPtr job, IntPtr process);
  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool TerminateJobObject(IntPtr job, uint code);
  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool TerminateProcess(IntPtr process, uint code);
  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern uint ResumeThread(IntPtr thread);
  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool SetHandleInformation(IntPtr handle, uint mask, uint flags);
  [DllImport("kernel32.dll")] static extern IntPtr GetStdHandle(int number);
  [DllImport("kernel32.dll")] static extern IntPtr GetCurrentProcess();
  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool DuplicateHandle(IntPtr source, IntPtr handle, IntPtr target, out IntPtr copy, uint access, bool inherit, uint options);
  [DllImport("kernel32.dll")] static extern bool CloseHandle(IntPtr handle);
  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", CharSet = CharSet.Unicode, SetLastError = true)]
  static extern bool CreateProcess(string exe, StringBuilder command, IntPtr processAttrs, IntPtr threadAttrs,
    bool inherit, uint flags, IntPtr env, string cwd, ref Startup startup, out ProcessInfo info);

  readonly IntPtr job;
  public Process Root { get; private set; }
  public RunnerStep(bool gpu) {
    job = CreateJobObject(IntPtr.Zero, IntPtr.Zero);
    if (job == IntPtr.Zero) throw new Win32Exception();
    if (!gpu) return; // A successful ordinary queue launcher may leave background work running.
    // JOBOBJECT_EXTENDED_LIMIT_INFORMATION, LimitFlags = JOB_OBJECT_LIMIT_KILL_ON_JOB_CLOSE.
    byte[] limits = new byte[IntPtr.Size == 8 ? 144 : 112];
    limits[17] = 0x20;
    if (!SetInformationJobObject(job, 9, limits, limits.Length)) {
      var error = new Win32Exception();
      CloseHandle(job);
      throw error;
    }
  }
  public void Start(string exe, string args, string cwd, string output, string error) {
    using (var stdout = new FileStream(output, FileMode.CreateNew, FileAccess.Write, FileShare.ReadWrite))
    using (var stderr = new FileStream(error, FileMode.CreateNew, FileAccess.Write, FileShare.ReadWrite)) {
      var startup = new Startup { size = Marshal.SizeOf(typeof(Startup)), flags = 0x100 }; // STARTF_USESTDHANDLES
      startup.output = stdout.SafeFileHandle.DangerousGetHandle();
      startup.error = stderr.SafeFileHandle.DangerousGetHandle();
      if (!SetHandleInformation(startup.output, 1, 1) || !SetHandleInformation(startup.error, 1, 1))
        throw new Win32Exception();
      // A caller may have no stdin (for example, the queue running under a service).
      IntPtr self = GetCurrentProcess();
      DuplicateHandle(self, GetStdHandle(-10), self, out startup.input, 0, true, 2);
      ProcessInfo info;
      try {
        var command = new StringBuilder("\"" + exe + "\" " + args);
        if (!CreateProcess(exe, command, IntPtr.Zero, IntPtr.Zero, true, 4, IntPtr.Zero, cwd, ref startup, out info))
          throw new Win32Exception();
      } finally {
        if (startup.input != IntPtr.Zero) CloseHandle(startup.input);
      }
      bool started = false;
      try {
        if (!AssignProcessToJobObject(job, info.process)) throw new Win32Exception();
        Root = Process.GetProcessById(info.pid);
        var handle = Root.Handle; // Keep the process identity and its exit code after exit.
        if (ResumeThread(info.thread) == UInt32.MaxValue) throw new Win32Exception();
        started = true;
      } finally {
        if (!started) TerminateProcess(info.process, 125);
        CloseHandle(info.thread);
        CloseHandle(info.process);
      }
    }
  }
  public Usage ReadUsage() {
    Usage usage;
    if (!QueryInformationJobObject(job, 1, out usage, Marshal.SizeOf(typeof(Usage)), IntPtr.Zero))
      throw new Win32Exception();
    return usage;
  }
  public void Kill() {
    if (!TerminateJobObject(job, 124)) throw new Win32Exception();
  }
  public void Dispose() {
    if (Root != null) Root.Dispose();
    CloseHandle(job);
  }
}

public sealed class RunnerStepFiles : IDisposable {
  readonly FileSystemWatcher watcher;
  readonly string folder;
  int changes;
  public RunnerStepFiles(string output) {
    if (String.IsNullOrEmpty(output)) return;
    folder = Path.GetFullPath(output).TrimEnd('\\', '/') + Path.DirectorySeparatorChar;
    string parent = folder;
    while (!Directory.Exists(parent)) parent = Path.GetDirectoryName(parent.TrimEnd('\\', '/'));
    watcher = new FileSystemWatcher(parent) { IncludeSubdirectories = true, NotifyFilter = NotifyFilters.FileName };
    watcher.Created += Changed;
    watcher.Renamed += Changed;
    watcher.Error += (sender, e) => Interlocked.Increment(ref changes); // An overflow also means file activity.
    watcher.EnableRaisingEvents = true;
  }
  void Changed(object sender, FileSystemEventArgs e) {
    if (e.FullPath.StartsWith(folder, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) Interlocked.Increment(ref changes);
  }
  public int Changes { get { return Volatile.Read(ref changes); } }
  public void Dispose() { if (watcher != null) watcher.Dispose(); }
}

public sealed class RunnerStepOutput : IDisposable {
  // One shared list for Godot, Blender and other steps. Keep the matched phrase in the log reason.
  static readonly Regex fatal = new Regex("Jolt Physics job system exceeded|Out of memory|Assertion failed", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase);
  static readonly Regex errorLine = new Regex(@"\b(error|fatal|exception|failed|failure|assertion)\b", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase);
  readonly FileStream source;
  readonly Stream target;
  readonly byte[] bytes = new byte[65536];
  readonly char[] chars = new char[65536];
  readonly Decoder decoder = Encoding.UTF8.GetDecoder();
  readonly StringBuilder line = new StringBuilder();
  string previousError;
  int repeats;
  bool longLine;
  public string KnownFatal { get; private set; }
  public bool RepeatedError { get; private set; }
  public bool Pending { get { return source.Position < source.Length; } }
  public RunnerStepOutput(string path, bool stderr) {
    source = new FileStream(path, FileMode.Open, FileAccess.Read, FileShare.ReadWrite);
    target = stderr ? Console.OpenStandardError() : Console.OpenStandardOutput();
  }
  public void ResetRepeats() { previousError = null; repeats = 0; RepeatedError = false; }
  public bool Drain(bool scan) {
    int count = source.Read(bytes, 0, bytes.Length);
    if (count == 0) return false;
    target.Write(bytes, 0, count);
    target.Flush();
    if (!scan) return true;
    int length = decoder.GetChars(bytes, 0, count, chars, 0);
    for (int i = 0; i < length; i++) {
      if (chars[i] == '\n') {
        string text = line.ToString().Trim();
        ScanFatal(text);
        if (longLine || !errorLine.IsMatch(text)) { previousError = null; repeats = 0; }
        else {
          repeats = text == previousError ? repeats + 1 : 1;
          previousError = text;
          if (repeats >= 50) RepeatedError = true;
        }
        line.Clear();
        longLine = false;
      } else if (line.Length == 8192) {
        ScanFatal(line.ToString());
        line.Remove(0, 4096);
        longLine = true;
        line.Append(chars[i]);
      } else line.Append(chars[i]);
    }
    ScanFatal(line.ToString());
    return true;
  }
  void ScanFatal(string text) {
    if (KnownFatal != null) return;
    var match = fatal.Match(text);
    if (match.Success) KnownFatal = match.Value;
  }
  public void Dispose() { source.Dispose(); }
}
'@
}
function Get-StepLimit($Value, $Default, $Name) {
  $number = $Default
  if ($Value -and ($Value -notmatch '^\d+$' -or -not [int]::TryParse($Value, [ref]$number) -or $number -lt 1)) {
    throw "$Name must be a positive number of seconds"
  }
  return $number
}
function Stop-StepTree($Job, $Log, $Step) {
  # Keep the claim while Windows still reports active processes, including during cleanup after a watchdog error.
  while (($usage = $Job.ReadUsage()).Active) {
    try { $Job.Kill() }
    catch { Write-KillLog $Log "$Step kill failed, retrying with GPU claim held: $($_.Exception.Message)" }
    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 50
  }
  Write-KillLog $Log "$Step kill verified: all $($usage.Total) processes gone (root PID $($Job.Root.Id))"
}
if ($LibraryOnly) { return }
$raw = $env:TMO_ARGS
if (-not $raw) { Write-Output 'usage: tmo.bat <seconds> <exe> [args ...]'; exit 125 }
$m = [regex]::Match($raw, '^\s*(\d+)\s+("([^"]+)"|(\S+))\s*(.*)$')
if (-not $m.Success) { Write-Output "run_timeout: cannot read the command line: $raw"; exit 125 }
$Seconds = [int]$m.Groups[1].Value
$Exe = if ($m.Groups[3].Success) { $m.Groups[3].Value } else { $m.Groups[4].Value }
$argLine = $m.Groups[5].Value
$root = Join-Path $env:USERPROFILE 'Downloads\voidsworn_test'
$logDir = Join-Path $root 'queue\logs'
if (-not (Test-Path $logDir)) { $logDir = $PSScriptRoot }
$log = Join-Path $logDir 'timeouts.log'
if ($env:PC_RUNNER_TEST -eq '1' -and $env:PC_TEST_TIMEOUT_LOG) { $log = $env:PC_TEST_TIMEOUT_LOG }
$watch = $env:TMO_REPO -and $env:TMO_OUT -and $env:TMO_LOG
$watchRepo = $env:TMO_REPO; $watchOut = $env:TMO_OUT
$stall = 30.0
if ($watch -and $env:STALL_MIN) { $stall = [double]::Parse($env:STALL_MIN, [Globalization.CultureInfo]::InvariantCulture) }
if ($Seconds -le 0 -or $stall -le 0 -or [double]::IsNaN($stall) -or [double]::IsInfinity($stall)) { throw 'ceiling and STALL_MIN must be positive' }
$logs = @($env:TMO_LOG, $env:TMO_ERR_LOG) | Where-Object { $_ }
if ($watch) {
  $before = Get-RunProgress $watchRepo $logs
  $base = $before.Head
  $bp = Join-Path $watchOut 'base_commit.txt'
  if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $bp) { $base = [IO.File]::ReadAllText($bp).Trim() }
  Initialize-RunTree
} else {
  Initialize-StepWatchdog
  $stepName = $env:TMO_STEP
  if (-not $stepName) { $stepName = [IO.Path]::GetFileName($Exe) }
  $stepOut = $env:TMO_STEP_OUT
  if (-not $stepOut) { $stepOut = $watchOut }
  if (-not $stepOut -and $argLine -match '(?:^|\s)--out(?:\s+|=)(?:"([^"]+)"|(\S+))') {
    $stepOut = if ($Matches[1]) { $Matches[1] } else { $Matches[2] }
  }
  $idleDefault = if ([IO.Path]::GetFileName($Exe) -match '^(Godot|Voidsworn)') { 300 } else { 600 }
  $idleSeconds = Get-StepLimit $env:TMO_IDLE_S $idleDefault 'TMO_IDLE_S'
  if ($env:PC_RUNNER_TEST -ne '1') { $idleSeconds = [Math]::Max($idleDefault, $idleSeconds) }
}
# Nov 1: a test job ran all through Ryan's first Godot playtest, slowing the PC and overwriting the game's log.
# Wait while Ryan is playing: the game is Voidsworn.exe or a windowed Godot started with no job arguments
# (play_godot.bat passes none; every job passes --headless, --script, --bot or similar). Waiting is not timed.
function Test-RyanPlaying {
  try { $ps = Get-CimInstance Win32_Process -Filter "Name LIKE 'Voidsworn%.exe' OR Name LIKE 'Godot%.exe'" } catch { return $false }
  foreach ($q in $ps) {
    if ($q.Name -like '*_console.exe') { continue }
    $cl = [string]$q.CommandLine
    $rest = [regex]::Replace($cl, '^\s*("[^"]*"|\S+)', '')
    $rest = [regex]::Replace($rest, '--path\s+("[^"]*"|\S+)', '')
    if ($rest -notmatch '--?\w') { return $true }
  }
  return $false
}
$w0 = Get-Date; $said = $false
# Codex jobs are light (one model session, small node scripts) and do not touch the game's log: they run during play.
$noWait = ($Exe -match 'codex(\.cmd|\.exe)?$') -or ($env:PC_RUNNER_TEST -eq '1')
while ((-not $noWait) -and (Test-RyanPlaying) -and ((Get-Date) - $w0).TotalHours -lt 8) {
  if (-not $said) { Write-Output "run_timeout: the game is open, waiting until it closes"; "$(Get-Date -Format s) WAIT game open: $Exe" | Out-File $log -Append -Encoding utf8; $said = $true }
  Start-Sleep -Seconds 20
}
if ($said) { "$(Get-Date -Format s) RESUME after $([int]((Get-Date) - $w0).TotalMinutes) min: $Exe" | Out-File $log -Append -Encoding utf8 }
# R293: acquire only around the GPU command, never the whole model session.
# A pending solo claim drains all shared slots and blocks new workers.
$gpuHelper = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'gpu_slots.js'
$gpuNode = 'node.exe'
$bundledNode = Join-Path $root 'node\node.exe'
if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $bundledNode) { $gpuNode = $bundledNode }
$gpuRoot = $root
if ($env:PC_RUNNER_TEST -eq '1' -and $env:PC_TEST_GPU_ROOT) { $gpuRoot = $env:PC_TEST_GPU_ROOT }
$gpuToken = ''
$canReleaseGpu = $true
try {
$classify = @{ exe = $Exe; args = $argLine; cwd = (Get-Location).Path; requested = [string]$env:PC_GPU_MODE; weight = [string]$env:PC_GPU_WEIGHT } | ConvertTo-Json -Compress
$gpuClass = ($classify | & $gpuNode $gpuHelper classify) -join ''
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'GPU command classification failed' }
$gpuClass = $gpuClass | ConvertFrom-Json
$gpuMode = $gpuClass.mode
$soloMax = 0
if ($gpuMode -and $watch) { throw 'a GPU claim must wrap one step; clear TMO_REPO, TMO_OUT and TMO_LOG' }
if ($gpuMode -eq 'solo') {
  $soloMax = Get-StepLimit $env:PC_GPU_SOLO_MAX_S 1200 'PC_GPU_SOLO_MAX_S'
}
if ($gpuMode) {
  Write-Output "GPU: waiting for $gpuMode claim, weight $($gpuClass.weight) (issue $env:PC_GPU_ISSUE)"
  # Admission has its own clock in gpu_slots.js (PC_GPU_WAIT_SECONDS, default 300).
  # Read the helper's diagnostic even under Windows PowerShell's native stderr handling.
  $ea = $ErrorActionPreference
  try {
    $ErrorActionPreference = 'Continue'
    & $gpuNode $gpuHelper acquire $gpuRoot ([string]$env:PC_GPU_ISSUE + ':gpu') $PID $gpuMode $gpuClass.weight 2>&1 | ForEach-Object {
      $line = [string]$_
      if ($line -eq 'WAIT') { Write-Host "GPU: still waiting for $gpuMode claim" }
      elseif ($line -cmatch '\A[0-9a-f]{8}(?:-[0-9a-f]{4}){3}-[0-9a-f]{12}\z') {
        $gpuToken = $line
        $claimTimer = [Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
      }
      else { Write-Output $line }
    }
    $admissionExit = $LASTEXITCODE
  } finally { $ErrorActionPreference = $ea }
  if ($admissionExit -ne 0 -or -not $gpuToken) {
    Write-Output 'GPU: admission failed; command was not started'
    exit 126
  }
  Write-Output "GPU: acquired $gpuMode claim $gpuToken"
}
# A forced claim applies to this command only, never a nested watchdog.
$env:PC_GPU_MODE = ''
$env:PC_GPU_WEIGHT = ''
$env:PC_GPU_SOLO_MAX_S = ''
$env:TMO_IDLE_S = ''; $env:TMO_STEP = ''; $env:TMO_STEP_OUT = ''
# Child commands may use tmo.bat too. Their own timeout must never overwrite this agent's resume note.
$env:TMO_REPO = ''; $env:TMO_OUT = ''; $env:TMO_LOG = ''; $env:TMO_ERR_LOG = ''
if (-not $watch) {
  $spool = Join-Path ([IO.Path]::GetTempPath()) ("runner-step-" + [Guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N'))
  $outPath = Join-Path $spool 'stdout.log'
  $errPath = Join-Path $spool 'stderr.log'
  $job = $files = $stdout = $stderr = $null
  $keepSpool = $true
  $finished = $false
  try {
    $cwd = (Get-Location).Path
    $target = if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $Exe -PathType Leaf) { (Get-Item -LiteralPath $Exe).FullName }
              else { (Get-Command -Name $Exe -CommandType Application | Select-Object -First 1).Source }
    $arguments = $argLine
    if ($target -match '\.(bat|cmd)$') {
      $arguments = '/d /s /c ""' + $target + '" ' + $argLine + '"'
      $target = $env:ComSpec
    }
    if ($stepOut) { $stepOut = $ExecutionContext.SessionState.Path.GetUnresolvedProviderPathFromPSPath($stepOut) }
    $files = [RunnerStepFiles]::new([string]$stepOut)
    $job = [RunnerStep]::new([bool]$gpuToken)
    $null = [IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory($spool)
    $timer = [Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
    $job.Start($target, $arguments, $cwd, $outPath, $errPath)
    $canReleaseGpu = $false
  } catch {
    # No child code runs unless Start attached it to the job and resumed it.
    if ($job -and $job.Root) { Stop-StepTree $job $log $stepName }
    if ($job) { $job.Dispose() }
    if ($files) { $files.Dispose() }
    Write-KillLog $log "START FAILED $stepName ($Exe): $($_.Exception.Message)"
    exit 125
  }
  try {
    $stdout = [RunnerStepOutput]::new($outPath, $false)
    $stderr = [RunnerStepOutput]::new($errPath, $true)
    $lastProgress = 0.0
    $lastCpu = 0
    $lastFiles = 0
    while ($true) {
      $readOut = $stdout.Drain($true)
      $readErr = $stderr.Drain($true)
      $fatal = if ($stdout.KnownFatal) { $stdout.KnownFatal } else { $stderr.KnownFatal }
      # The two streams have separate handles. Any output on the other stream breaks a repeat streak.
      if ($readOut) { $stderr.ResetRepeats() }
      if ($readErr) { $stdout.ResetRepeats() }
      $elapsed = $timer.Elapsed.TotalSeconds
      $usage = $job.ReadUsage()
      $fileCount = $files.Changes
      if ($readOut -or $readErr -or $usage.CpuTime -gt $lastCpu -or $fileCount -ne $lastFiles) {
        $lastProgress = $elapsed
      }
      $lastCpu = $usage.CpuTime
      $lastFiles = $fileCount
      $pendingOutput = $stdout.Pending -or $stderr.Pending
      if ($fatal) { $reason = "FATAL $fatal"; break }
      if ($stdout.RepeatedError -or $stderr.RepeatedError) { $reason = 'FATAL repeated error line 50 times'; break }
      if ($soloMax -and $claimTimer.Elapsed.TotalSeconds -ge $soloMax) { $reason = "SOLO CAP after $soloMax s"; break }
      if ($job.Root.HasExited -and (-not $gpuToken -or -not $usage.Active) -and -not $pendingOutput) {
        $finished = $true
        $keepSpool = [bool]$usage.Active
        if ($keepSpool) { Write-Output "run_timeout: background output continues in $spool" }
        exit $job.Root.ExitCode
      }
      if ($elapsed -ge $Seconds) { $reason = 'CEILING'; break }
      if ($elapsed - $lastProgress -ge $idleSeconds) { $reason = "IDLE after $idleSeconds s without output, new files or CPU progress"; break }
      if (-not $pendingOutput) {
        $waitMs = [Math]::Min(250, ($Seconds - $elapsed) * 1000)
        $waitMs = [Math]::Min($waitMs, ($idleSeconds - ($elapsed - $lastProgress)) * 1000)
        if ($soloMax) { $waitMs = [Math]::Min($waitMs, ($soloMax - $claimTimer.Elapsed.TotalSeconds) * 1000) }
        Start-Sleep -Milliseconds ([int][Math]::Max(1, $waitMs))
      }
    }
    $stoppedAfter = [Math]::Round($elapsed, 3)
    Stop-StepTree $job $log $stepName
    $canReleaseGpu = $true
    $finished = $true
    # Descendants have closed their streams. Copy the last bytes before reporting the stop.
    while ($stdout.Drain($false)) { }
    while ($stderr.Drain($false)) { }
    Write-KillLog $log "TIMEOUT $stepName ($Exe): $reason at $stoppedAfter s (ceiling $Seconds s). Child logs: $spool"
    exit 124
  } finally {
    if (-not $finished) { Stop-StepTree $job $log $stepName }
    $canReleaseGpu = $true
    if ($stdout) { $stdout.Dispose() }
    if ($stderr) { $stderr.Dispose() }
    $files.Dispose()
    $job.Dispose()
    if (-not $keepSpool) {
      # Only delete these two files and the empty scratch directory we created.
      try {
        [IO.File]::Delete($outPath)
        [IO.File]::Delete($errPath)
        [IO.Directory]::Delete($spool)
      } catch { Write-Output "run_timeout: child log cleanup failed in $spool : $($_.Exception.Message)" }
    }
  }
}
# Outer agents keep their original separate logs and repo/commit progress checks.
$timer = [Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
$lastProgress = 0.0
$scanEvery = 60.0
if ($env:PC_RUNNER_TEST -eq '1' -and $env:PC_TEST_SCAN_S) { $scanEvery = [double]$env:PC_TEST_SCAN_S }
$nextScan = $scanEvery
try {
  if ($argLine -ne '') { $p = Start-Process -FilePath $Exe -ArgumentList $argLine -NoNewWindow -PassThru }
  else { $p = Start-Process -FilePath $Exe -NoNewWindow -PassThru }
} catch {
  "$(Get-Date -Format s) START FAILED $Exe $argLine : $_" | Out-File $log -Append -Encoding utf8
  Write-Output "run_timeout: could not start $Exe : $_"
  exit 125
}
$null = $p.Handle   # keeps the exit code readable after the process ends
$tree = @{ }; $tree[$p.Id] = $p
$completed = $false
try {
$poll = 5000
if ($env:PC_RUNNER_TEST -eq '1' -and $env:PC_TEST_POLL_MS) { $poll = [int]$env:PC_TEST_POLL_MS }
while ($true) {
  Update-RunTree $tree
  $remaining = [Math]::Max(1, [Math]::Min($poll, ($Seconds - $timer.Elapsed.TotalSeconds) * 1000))
  if ($p.WaitForExit([int]$remaining)) { $p.WaitForExit(); $completed = $true; exit $p.ExitCode }
  # Scan the checkout once a minute to avoid walking a large repo on every poll.
  if ($timer.Elapsed.TotalSeconds -ge $nextScan) {
    $after = Get-RunProgress $watchRepo $logs
    if (Test-RunProgress $before $after) { $lastProgress = $timer.Elapsed.TotalSeconds }
    $before = $after
    $nextScan = $timer.Elapsed.TotalSeconds + $scanEvery
  }
  $reason = Get-StopReason $timer.Elapsed.TotalSeconds ($timer.Elapsed.TotalSeconds - $lastProgress) $Seconds $stall
  if ($p.HasExited) { $p.WaitForExit(); $completed = $true; exit $p.ExitCode }
  if ($reason) { break }
}
$secs = [int]$timer.Elapsed.TotalSeconds
Stop-RunTree $p $tree $log
Save-RunResume $watchRepo $watchOut $logs $reason $base
"$(Get-Date -Format s) TIMEOUT $reason after $secs s (ceiling $Seconds s), verified dead tree of PID $($p.Id): $Exe $argLine" | Out-File $log -Append -Encoding utf8
Write-Output "run_timeout: TIMEOUT $reason after $secs s, killed $Exe (PID $($p.Id)) and its children"
exit 124
} finally {
  # Reap on an unexpected watchdog error too. A successful queue launcher may deliberately start background jobs.
  if (-not $completed) {
    Update-RunTree $tree
    if (@($tree.Values | Where-Object { -not $_.HasExited }).Count) { Stop-RunTree $p $tree $log }
  }
  foreach ($child in $tree.Values) { $child.Dispose() }
}
} finally {
  if ($gpuToken) {
    if ($canReleaseGpu) {
      & $gpuNode $gpuHelper release $gpuRoot $gpuToken
      if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { Write-Output "GPU: release failed; dead owner PID $PID will be reclaimed" }
      else { Write-Output "GPU: released $gpuMode claim $gpuToken" }
    }
  }
}

'use strict';
// Issues 818 and 1192: portable runner checks and real Windows watchdog, process-tree and GPU claim probes.
const assert = require('assert');
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const os = require('os');
const { spawnSync } = require('child_process');
const pc = path.resolve(__dirname, '../../../tools/pc');
const source = fs.readFileSync(path.join(pc, 'run_timeout.ps1'), 'utf8');

// Run the actual small PowerShell decision on every CI host. Only its arithmetic and variable syntax need translation.
const body = source.match(/function Get-StopReason\([^\n]+\) \{([\s\S]*?)\n\}/)[1];
const defaultStall = Number(source.match(/\$stall = (\d+(?:\.\d+)?)/)[1]);
assert.equal(defaultStall, 30);
const decide = new Function('Elapsed', 'Idle', 'Ceiling', 'StallMinutes', body
  .replace(/\$(Elapsed|Idle|Ceiling|StallMinutes)\b/g, '$1').replace(/ -ge /g, ' >= ')
  .replace('"STALLED after StallMinutes min"', '`STALLED after ${StallMinutes} min`'));
let passed = 0;
function pass(name) { passed++; console.log('PASS ' + name); }
function fakeRun(progressEvery, ceiling = 21600, stallMinutes = defaultStall) {
  let last = 0;
  for (let now = 0; now <= ceiling; now += 60) {
    if (progressEvery && now % progressEvery === 0) last = now;
    const reason = decide(now, now - last, ceiling, stallMinutes);
    if (reason) return { now, reason };
    if (now === 7260) assert.equal(reason, '', 'a busy run past two hours must continue');
  }
  throw new Error('no stop at ceiling');
}
assert.deepEqual(fakeRun(60), { now: 21600, reason: 'CEILING' });
pass('busy fake run passes two hours and stops only at six-hour ceiling');
assert.deepEqual(fakeRun(0), { now: 1800, reason: 'STALLED after 30 min' });
pass('silent fake run stops at the stall window');
assert.deepEqual(fakeRun(60, 10800), { now: 10800, reason: 'CEILING' });
assert.deepEqual(fakeRun(0, 21600, 10), { now: 600, reason: 'STALLED after 10 min' });
assert.equal(decide(21600, 1800, 21600, 30), 'CEILING');
assert.equal(decide(1799, 1799, 21600, 30), '');
pass('ceiling and stall overrides, exact boundaries, ceiling takes priority');

for (const [runner, cap] of [['codex_issue', 'CODEX_CAP'], ['work_issue', 'WORK_CAP']]) {
  const bat = fs.readFileSync(path.join(pc, runner + '.bat'), 'utf8');
  assert(bat.includes(`if not defined ${cap} set "${cap}=21600"`));
  assert(bat.includes('if not defined PC_GPU_WAIT_SECONDS set "PC_GPU_WAIT_SECONDS=1800"'));
  for (const name of ['TMO_REPO', 'TMO_OUT', 'TMO_LOG']) {
    assert(bat.includes(`set "${name}=`), runner + ' must set ' + name);
    assert(bat.includes(`set "${name}="`), runner + ' must clear ' + name);
  }
  assert(bat.includes('stop_reason.txt'));
  assert(bat.includes('if defined PARTIAL goto :haschange'));
}
pass('both launchers set agent progress paths, preserve ceiling overrides and keep partial runs with no file changes');

const ship = fs.readFileSync(path.join(pc, 'jobs', 'ship_job.bat'), 'utf8');
const recipe = path.resolve(pc, '../recipe');
const limits = fs.readFileSync(path.join(recipe, 'env.bat'), 'utf8');
for (const [file, settings] of [[ship, ship], ...fs.readdirSync(recipe)
  .filter(name => /^[1-7]_.*\.bat$|^install_godot\.bat$/.test(name))
  .map(name => [fs.readFileSync(path.join(recipe, name), 'utf8'), limits])]) {
  for (const line of file.split(/\r?\n/).filter(line => /\bcall\b.*tmo\.bat/i.test(line))) {
    const cap = line.match(/tmo\.bat" %([A-Z_]+_CAP)% /i);
    assert(cap, 'give the step a named cap: ' + line);
    const seconds = settings.match(new RegExp('set "' + cap[1] + '=(\\d+)"', 'ig'));
    assert.equal(seconds?.length, 1, 'define the cap once: ' + cap[1]);
    assert(Number(seconds[0].match(/=(\d+)/)[1]) <= 3600, 'oversized step: ' + line);
  }
}
assert(ship.includes('if not defined PC_GPU_WAIT_SECONDS set PC_GPU_WAIT_SECONDS=3600'));
pass('ship and recipe watchdog calls use named step caps and ship GPU admission keeps its 3600 s default');

if (process.platform !== 'win32') {
  console.log('SKIP Windows process and file probes: Windows standing checks run them');
  console.log(`PASS runner stall checks (${passed} groups)`);
  process.exit(0);
}
const root = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'runner818 '));
const pidFiles = [], helpers = [];
function alive(pid) {
  try { process.kill(pid, 0); return true; }
  catch (e) { if (e.code === 'ESRCH') return false; throw e; }
}
function agentPids() {
  return pidFiles.flatMap(file => fs.existsSync(file) ? fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8').trim().split(/\r?\n/).filter(Boolean).map(Number) : []);
}
// A failing assertion or a timed-out PowerShell must not leave the very processes this check is testing.
try {
const script = path.join(root, 'probes.ps1');
fs.writeFileSync(script, `
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
. (Join-Path $args[0] 'run_timeout.ps1') -LibraryOnly
function Assert($ok, $label) { if (-not $ok) { throw $label } }

// Actual command launch, process-tree termination, stop reason and resume note. All output stays in TEMP.
for (const scenario of ['silent', 'busy', 'lying-killer', 'launcher-exit']) {
  const busy = scenario === 'busy';
  const folder = path.join(root, scenario);
  fs.mkdirSync(folder);
  const fake = path.join(folder, 'agent.js');
  const pidFile = path.join(folder, 'pids.txt'); pidFiles.push(pidFile);
  const cleanChild = "if (['TMO_REPO','TMO_OUT','TMO_LOG','TMO_ERR_LOG'].some(k=>process.env[k])) throw Error('watch settings leaked to child'); ";
  const recordPid = `require('fs').appendFileSync(${JSON.stringify(pidFile)}, process.pid+'\\n'); `;
  // Busy and early-exit launchers also own a child and a grandchild, not merely a single sleeping process.
  const leaf = path.join(folder, 'leaf.js'), child = path.join(folder, 'child.js');
  fs.writeFileSync(leaf, recordPid + 'setInterval(()=>{},100);');
  fs.writeFileSync(child, recordPid + `require('child_process').spawn(process.execPath,[${JSON.stringify(leaf)}],{stdio:'inherit',windowsHide:true}); setInterval(()=>{},100);`);
  const nested = ['busy', 'launcher-exit'].includes(scenario) ? `require('child_process').spawn(process.execPath,[${JSON.stringify(child)}],{stdio:'inherit',windowsHide:true,detached:true}); ` : '';
  fs.writeFileSync(fake, cleanChild + recordPid + nested + (busy ? "console.log('working'); setInterval(()=>console.log('still working'),100);" : "console.log('last action: waiting'); setInterval(()=>{},100);") +
    (scenario === 'launcher-exit' ? 'setTimeout(()=>process.exit(7),1500);' : ''));
  const killer = path.join(folder, 'taskkill.bat');
  fs.writeFileSync(killer, '@echo off\r\necho stub taskkill ' + (busy ? 'Access denied' : 'false success') + '\r\nexit /b ' + (busy ? '5' : '0') + '\r\n');
  const log = path.join(folder, 'log.txt');
  const fd = fs.openSync(log, 'w');
  const reason = busy ? 'CEILING' : 'STALLED after 0.02 min';
  let run;
  try { run = spawnSync('powershell.exe', ['-NoProfile', '-ExecutionPolicy', 'Bypass', '-File', path.join(pc, 'run_timeout.ps1')], {
    cwd: folder, env: { ...process.env, TMO_ARGS: `4 "${process.execPath}" "${fake}"`,
      TMO_REPO: path.join(root, 'repo'), TMO_OUT: folder, TMO_LOG: log, TMO_ERR_LOG: '',
      STALL_MIN: scenario === 'launcher-exit' ? '1' : '0.02', PC_RUNNER_TEST: '1', PC_TEST_POLL_MS: '100', PC_TEST_SCAN_S: '0.1', PC_TEST_TIMEOUT_LOG: path.join(root, 'timeouts.log'),
      PC_TEST_TASKKILL: ['busy', 'lying-killer'].includes(scenario) ? killer : '' },
    stdio: ['ignore', fd, fd], windowsHide: true, timeout: 30000
  }); } finally { fs.closeSync(fd); }
  helpers.push(run.pid);
  const output = fs.readFileSync(log, 'utf8');
  assert.equal(run.status, scenario === 'launcher-exit' ? 7 : 124, output + String(run.error || ''));
  const pids = fs.readFileSync(pidFile, 'utf8').trim().split(/\r?\n/).map(Number);
  assert.equal(pids.length, ['busy', 'launcher-exit'].includes(scenario) ? 3 : 1);
  if (scenario === 'launcher-exit') {
    assert(!alive(pids[0]) && pids.slice(1).every(alive), 'ordinary queue launchers must be able to leave background work running');
    for (const pid of pids.slice(1).reverse()) process.kill(pid, 'SIGKILL');
    const deadline = Date.now() + 5000;
    while (pids.some(alive) && Date.now() < deadline) Atomics.wait(new Int32Array(new SharedArrayBuffer(4)), 0, 0, 50);
    assert(pids.every(pid => !alive(pid)), 'background fixture cleanup failed');
    pass('normal launcher keeps exit 7 and background jobs; the test reaps its child and grandchild'); continue;
  }
  assert(pids.every(pid => !alive(pid)), scenario + ': fake agents survived: ' + pids);
  const verified = output.match(/kill verified: all (\d+) processes gone \(PIDs ([\d, ]+)\)/);
  assert(verified && Number(verified[1]) >= pids.length && pids.every(pid => verified[2].split(', ').map(Number).includes(pid)), output);
  if (busy) assert(/taskkill PID \d+ exit 5:/.test(output) && output.includes('Access denied'), output);
  if (scenario === 'lying-killer') assert(/taskkill PID \d+ exit 0:/.test(output), output);
  assert.equal(fs.readFileSync(path.join(folder, 'stop_reason.txt'), 'utf8'), reason);
  const note = fs.readFileSync(path.join(folder, 'resume.txt'), 'utf8');
  assert(note.includes(reason) && note.includes(busy ? 'still working' : 'last action: waiting'));
  pass(`Windows ${scenario}: verifies every agent is gone, saves reason and tail, logs taskkill and fallback result`);
}
const timeoutLog = fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, 'timeouts.log'), 'utf8');
assert(timeoutLog.includes('CEILING') && timeoutLog.includes('STALLED after 0.02 min'));
const ordinary = path.join(root, 'ordinary.js');

// A fake Godot executable uses Windows processes and the real claim helper. Its GPU go file and claims live in TEMP.
const game = path.join(root, 'Godot_stall_fixture_console.exe');
const gameSource = path.join(root, 'FakeGodot.cs');
fs.writeFileSync(gameSource, String.raw`
using System;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.Threading;
public static class FakeGodot {
  static void StartChild(string exe, string role, string output) {
    Process.Start(new ProcessStartInfo(exe, role + " \"" + output + "\"") {
      UseShellExecute = false, CreateNoWindow = true
    });
  }
  public static int Main(string[] args) {
    string mode = args[0], output = args[1];
    string exe = typeof(FakeGodot).Assembly.Location;
    File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(output, "pid-" + mode), Process.GetCurrentProcess().Id + "\n");
    if (mode == "leaf") { Thread.Sleep(Timeout.Infinite); return 0; }
    if (mode == "branch") {
      StartChild(exe, "leaf", output);
      Thread.Sleep(Timeout.Infinite);
      return 0;
    }
    if (mode == "new-files" || mode == "cpu") {
      var clock = Stopwatch.StartNew();
      int file = 0;
      while (clock.ElapsedMilliseconds < 1800) {
        if (mode == "cpu") Thread.SpinWait(10000);
        else {
          File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(output, "work-" + file++), "progress");
          Thread.Sleep(150);
        }
      }
      return 7;
    }
    if (mode == "mixed") {
      for (int i = 0; i < 60; i++) {
        Console.Error.WriteLine("ERROR: fixture retry");
        Console.WriteLine("other output");
      }
      return 7;
    }
    StartChild(exe, "branch", output);
    if (!SpinWait.SpinUntil(() => File.Exists(Path.Combine(output, "pid-leaf")), 5000)) return 9;
    string claim = Path.Combine(output, "gpu", "gpu_slot_1");
    if (mode == "idle" || mode == "solo") {
      if (!File.Exists(claim) || !File.ReadAllText(claim).Contains("1192")) return 8;
      Console.WriteLine("fixture saw held GPU claim");
    }
    if (mode == "idle") Console.Error.Write("last output before the hang");
    if (mode == "jolt") Console.Error.Write("Jolt Physics job system exceeded the maximum number of jobs");
    if (mode == "repeat") for (int i = 0; i < 50; i++) Console.WriteLine("ERROR: fixture retry");
    if (mode == "solo") {
      Console.WriteLine("launcher exited while descendants were still running");
      return 7;
    }
    Thread.Sleep(Timeout.Infinite);
    return 0;
  }
}
`);

for (const [mode, reason] of [
  ['idle', 'IDLE after 1 s'], ['jolt', 'FATAL Jolt Physics job system exceeded'],
  ['repeat', 'FATAL repeated error line 50 times'], ['solo', 'SOLO CAP after 2 s'],
  ['new-files', ''], ['cpu', ''], ['mixed', '']
]) {
  const folder = path.join(root, 'step-' + mode);
  const gpu = path.join(folder, 'gpu');
  fs.mkdirSync(gpu, { recursive: true });
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(gpu, 'gpu_go.txt'), 'GPU cleared');
  for (const role of [mode, 'branch', 'leaf']) pidFiles.push(path.join(folder, 'pid-' + role));
  const stepLog = path.join(folder, 'timeouts.log');
  const claimed = mode === 'idle' || mode === 'solo';
  const flags = claimed ? '--bench' : '--headless';
  const start = Date.now();
  const run = spawnSync(process.env.ComSpec || 'cmd.exe', ['/d', '/s', '/c',
    `""${tmo}" 12 "${game}" ${mode} "${folder}" ${flags}"`], {
    cwd: folder, encoding: 'utf8', windowsHide: true, windowsVerbatimArguments: true, timeout: 15000,
    env: { ...process.env, TMO_REPO: '', TMO_OUT: '', TMO_LOG: '', TMO_ERR_LOG: '', TMO_STEP: 'fake Godot ' + mode,
      TMO_STEP_OUT: folder, TMO_IDLE_S: mode === 'solo' ? '10' : '1', PC_GPU_SOLO_MAX_S: '2',
      PC_GPU_LANE: '', PC_GPU_ISSUE: '1192', PC_GPU_WAIT_SECONDS: '5', PC_GPU_MODE: '', PC_GPU_WEIGHT: '',
      PC_RUNNER_TEST: '1', PC_TEST_GPU_ROOT: gpu, PC_TEST_TIMEOUT_LOG: stepLog }
  });
  helpers.push(run.pid);
  const diagnostic = run.stdout + run.stderr + String(run.error || '');
  assert.equal(run.status, reason ? 124 : 7, diagnostic);
  if (!reason) { pass('fake Godot ' + mode + ' preserves normal completion'); continue; }
  const details = fs.readFileSync(stepLog, 'utf8');
  const stopped = details.match(/ at (\d+(?:\.\d+)?) s \(ceiling 12 s\)/);
  assert(stopped && details.includes('TIMEOUT fake Godot ' + mode) && details.includes(reason), details);
  assert(Number(details.match(/kill verified: all (\d+) processes gone/)?.[1]) >= 3, details);
  const pids = [mode, 'branch', 'leaf'].map(role => Number(fs.readFileSync(path.join(folder, 'pid-' + role), 'utf8')));
  assert(pids.every(pid => pid > 0 && !alive(pid)), 'fake Godot tree survived: ' + pids);
  if (reason.startsWith('FATAL')) assert(Date.now() - start < 15000, 'fatal abort took 15 seconds or more');
  if (claimed) {
    assert(run.stdout.includes('fixture saw held GPU claim'), diagnostic);
    const verifiedAt = run.stdout.indexOf('kill verified:');
    assert(verifiedAt >= 0 && run.stdout.indexOf('GPU: released solo claim') > verifiedAt, diagnostic);
    for (const name of ['gpu_solo', 'gpu_slot_1']) assert.equal(fs.readFileSync(path.join(gpu, name), 'utf8'), '');
  }
  if (mode === 'idle') {
    assert(Number(stopped[1]) < 2, details);
    assert(run.stderr.includes('last output before the hang'), diagnostic);
    const spool = details.match(/Child logs: ([^\r\n]+)/)[1];
    assert(fs.readFileSync(path.join(spool, 'stderr.log'), 'utf8').includes('last output before the hang'));
  }
  pass(`fake Godot ${mode} stopped at ${stopped[1]} s, tree gone${claimed ? ', then GPU claim released' : ''}`);
}
} finally {
  for (const pid of [...agentPids().reverse(), ...helpers].filter(Boolean)) {
    if (alive(pid)) process.kill(pid, 'SIGKILL');
  }
  // Wait for forced cleanup on failure too. Never trust a kill's return value alone.
  const deadline = Date.now() + 5000;
  while (agentPids().some(alive) && Date.now() < deadline) Atomics.wait(new Int32Array(new SharedArrayBuffer(4)), 0, 0, 50);
  assert(agentPids().every(pid => !alive(pid)), 'test cleanup leaked fake agents');
}
pass('final cleanup asserts none of this test run\'s fake agents is alive');

@echo off
rem Run a program with a time limit: tmo.bat <seconds> <exe> [args ...]
rem Kills the whole process tree on ceiling, idle, or a fatal step log line (exit 124, reason and child logs in timeouts.log).
rem Otherwise returns the program's exit code. Stdout and stderr still go to the caller, so > log.txt 2>&1 captures both.
rem TMO_STEP names this step in timeouts.log. TMO_STEP_OUT watches for new files (defaults to --out, if supplied).
rem The step idle limit is 300 s for Godot and 600 s for other tools. TMO_IDLE_S may raise it for a single step.
rem For agent runs set TMO_REPO, TMO_OUT and TMO_LOG. The seconds argument is then the hard ceiling (runners default
rem to 21600), and STALL_MIN (default 30) stops a run only after no file, commit or log progress. TMO_ERR_LOG is optional.
rem A stopped agent writes resume.txt and stop_reason.txt in TMO_OUT. Clear these TMO variables before runner checks.
rem R293: shared capacity is in Downloads\voidsworn_test\gpu_slots.json, e.g. {"sharedSlots":4}, default 2.
rem Godot GPU commands weigh 1, Blender commands weigh 2; PC_GPU_WEIGHT overrides the weight for this command.
rem Timing scripts take solo once per command and wait for all slots. PC_GPU_SOLO_MAX_S defaults to 1200 s per claim.
rem For other GPU executables set PC_GPU_MODE=shared, or solo for timing. Waiting does not spend the command's limit.
setlocal EnableExtensions DisableDelayedExpansion
set "TMO_ARGS=%*"
powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File "%~dp0run_timeout.ps1"
exit /b %ERRORLEVEL%

commit 1df9aa74fdbbd93cd14724900ad4ddd687916d00
Author: Ryan Bailey <rcbailey12@gmail.com>
Date:   Thu Nov 19 12:09:46 2026 -0600

    Codex (issue #1193): GPU slots heal themselves: atomic writes, PID checks, no release on failed kill, no anonymous claims
    
    Implemented issue #1193. `tools/pc/gpu_slots.js` now writes claims atomically, archives damaged slot files, and checks PID, start time, and executable name. Claims require an issue, job directory, or script name.
    
    `tools/pc/run_timeout.ps1` records the child before it can use the GPU. A failed kill logs the surviving PIDs and retains the claim until those processes exit. Updated `docs/RULEBOOK.md` §3.4 and added regression coverage in `contrib/opus/tools/gpu_slots_tests.js`.
    
    All 21 GPU slot test groups, 11 runner stall groups, and the CPU-only `tmo.bat` smoke test passed on Windows. The standing suite reported two known baseline results and one sandbox hard-link error. The affected export test passed when rerun with Node in the writable temp folder.
    
    Drain GPU jobs before replacing both tools across the shared copies. Changes are uncommitted.
