@echo off
rem Issue 1174: the one shared ship build job. Every helper's ship run is a three-line wrapper that sets the inputs and calls
rem this file (docs/ship_pipeline/ship_job/README.md). Built from issue 1157's S10_RUN_1157.bat, the best guarded job of Nov 19.
rem
rem Inputs (environment, set by the wrapper):
rem   SHIP        the recipe ship id, the file name in tools\recipe\ships without .json (voidsworn_S10)
rem   CODE        the game folder the install writes (V16_37)
rem   BRANCH      the branch to build (opus/issue-1157)
rem   JOB_DIR     this job's own folder under Downloads\voidsworn_test (opus1157)
rem   STAGES      optional: the recipe stages, in order (default 1_concept_in to 7_export, all seven)
rem   SETS        optional: the a4_shots sets (default battle,close)
rem   FACS        optional: the a4_shots faction (default the part of SHIP before its first underscore)
rem   PAINT_BASE  optional: a branch merged in after the checkout; the pictures are then a labelled PREVIEW of that stack
rem   PC_GPU_WAIT_SECONDS  optional: how long each GPU step waits for its claim (default 3600, the most gpu_slots.js
rem               allows; the old 300 s default failed 1155, 1157 and 1158 on Nov 19 when several helpers queued at once)
rem
rem What it does, in order. Any failure skips every later step, the checks and the shots (the recipe folder is still copied
rem back, step 7, for the logs), and the last two lines of out\result.txt are always "done PASS" or "done FAILED: <step>" and then "all done".
rem   1. runs from its own copy in JOB_DIR (cmd reads a .bat as it goes, so a checkout must never rewrite the running file)
rem   2. its own clone JOB_DIR\repo (depth 50), the branch checked out (install files restored after a
rem      saved diff, never a forced checkout; any other local change or file collision stops the job), PAINT_BASE merged if set, a commit= line
rem   3. the headless import, then each stage logged with its exit code (stage 7 installs into the clone's own godot folder,
rem      never into Ryan's game)
rem   4. the installed glb must be this run's (written after the run started), else the install counts as failed
rem   5. the GPU readback, the authored interiors check and the death seam check on the installed glb; all three must pass
rem   6. the in-game shots at camera 170 and 60, only when everything above passed
rem   7. the recipe folder copied (never moved) into out\recipe
rem Writes only under voidsworn_test\JOB_DIR and voidsworn_test\recipe\SHIP (the stages' own folder). Deletes nothing: an earlier
rem run's out folder is renamed out_before_<time>, never removed.
if "%~1"=="ship" goto ship
setlocal EnableExtensions
set "V=%USERPROFILE%\Downloads\voidsworn_test"
set MISSING=
if not defined SHIP set MISSING=SHIP
if not defined CODE set MISSING=%MISSING% CODE
if not defined BRANCH set MISSING=%MISSING% BRANCH
if not defined JOB_DIR set MISSING=%MISSING% JOB_DIR
if defined MISSING (
  echo ship_job.bat: set %MISSING% first. See docs\ship_pipeline\ship_job\README.md for the three-line wrapper.
  exit /b 2
)
set "J=%V%\%JOB_DIR%"
if not exist "%J%" mkdir "%J%"
rem 1. run from this job's own copy
if /i not "%~f0"=="%J%\ship_job_running.bat" (
  copy /y "%~f0" "%J%\ship_job_running.bat" > nul
  call "%J%\ship_job_running.bat"
  exit /b
)
if not defined STAGES set "STAGES=1_concept_in 2_shape_ref 3_trace_hull 4_parts_kit 5_paint_bake 6_lod 7_export"
if not defined SETS set "SETS=battle,close"
if not defined FACS for /f "delims=_" %%F in ("%SHIP%") do set "FACS=%%F"
if not defined PC_GPU_WAIT_SECONDS set PC_GPU_WAIT_SECONDS=3600
if not defined PC_GPU_ISSUE set "PC_GPU_ISSUE=%JOB_DIR%"
set "SHIP_IMPORT_CAP=900"
set "SHIP_CHECK_CAP=600"
set "SHIP_SHOTS_CAP=600"
set "C=%J%\repo"
set "O=%J%\out"
for /f "usebackq delims=" %%T in (`powershell -NoProfile -Command "(Get-Date).ToString('yyyyMMdd_HHmmss')"`) do set "STAMP=%%T"
if exist "%O%" ren "%O%" "out_before_%STAMP%"
mkdir "%O%"
set "RES=%O%\result.txt"
for /f "usebackq delims=" %%T in (`powershell -NoProfile -Command "[DateTime]::UtcNow.Ticks"`) do set "START_TICKS=%%T"
echo start %SHIP% %CODE% %BRANCH% %DATE% %TIME% > "%RES%"
echo inputs STAGES=%STAGES% SETS=%SETS% FACS=%FACS% PAINT_BASE=%PAINT_BASE% PC_GPU_WAIT_SECONDS=%PC_GPU_WAIT_SECONDS% >> "%RES%"
if exist "%J%\out_before_%STAMP%" echo the earlier run's out folder is now out_before_%STAMP% >> "%RES%"
set FAILED=
set "GD=%V%\godot\Godot_v4.7.2-stable_win64_console.exe"

rem 2. own clone, the branch, PAINT_BASE
if not exist "%C%\.git" git clone -q --depth 50 https://github.com/rcbailey12/voidsworn "%C%" >> "%RES%" 2>&1
git -C "%C%" fetch -q --depth 50 origin %BRANCH% >> "%RES%" 2>&1
if errorlevel 1 set "FAILED=fetch %BRANCH%"
if defined FAILED goto collect
rem the checkout, in this job's own clone: stage 7 rewrites the tracked godot\assets\ships files here (manifest.json, the CODE
rem folder), and a plain checkout then refuses every rerun after the branch moved. So: list and save every tracked change
rem (clone_changes_before_checkout.txt and .patch); stop on a tracked change anywhere else (a hand fix in the clone); restore
rem only godot\assets\ships from the clone's own commit; then a plain checkout, never forced, that also refuses to overwrite
rem untracked or ignored files, so a local file the branch now tracks stops the job instead of being replaced (Artoo, PR 1179).
git -C "%C%" status --porcelain --untracked-files=no > "%O%\clone_changes_before_checkout.txt" 2> "%O%\log_clone_status.txt"
git -C "%C%" diff --binary HEAD > "%O%\clone_changes_before_checkout.patch" 2>> "%O%\log_clone_status.txt"
findstr /v /r /c:"^.. godot/assets/ships/" "%O%\clone_changes_before_checkout.txt" > "%O%\clone_changes_unexpected.txt"
for %%Z in ("%O%\clone_changes_unexpected.txt") do if %%~zZ GTR 0 set "FAILED=checkout: the clone has local changes outside godot\assets\ships, listed in clone_changes_unexpected.txt and saved in clone_changes_before_checkout.patch; nothing was reset"
if defined FAILED goto collect
for %%Z in ("%O%\clone_changes_before_checkout.txt") do if %%~zZ GTR 0 echo restoring the clone's install files listed in clone_changes_before_checkout.txt, saved first in clone_changes_before_checkout.patch >> "%RES%"
git -C "%C%" checkout -q HEAD -- godot/assets/ships >> "%RES%" 2>&1
if errorlevel 1 set "FAILED=checkout: could not restore godot\assets\ships in the clone"
if defined FAILED goto collect
git -C "%C%" checkout -q --no-overwrite-ignore -B shipjob FETCH_HEAD >> "%RES%" 2>&1
if errorlevel 1 set "FAILED=checkout %BRANCH%"
if defined FAILED goto collect
if not defined PAINT_BASE goto based
git -C "%C%" fetch -q --depth 50 origin %PAINT_BASE% >> "%RES%" 2>&1
if errorlevel 1 set "FAILED=fetch PAINT_BASE %PAINT_BASE%"
if defined FAILED goto collect
git -C "%C%" -c user.name=%JOB_DIR% -c user.email=%JOB_DIR%@localhost merge -q --no-edit --no-overwrite-ignore FETCH_HEAD >> "%RES%" 2>&1
if errorlevel 1 set "FAILED=merge PAINT_BASE %PAINT_BASE%"
if defined FAILED git -C "%C%" merge --abort >> "%RES%" 2>&1
if defined FAILED goto collect
echo merged %PAINT_BASE%: the pictures of this run are a PREVIEW of that paint stack >> "%RES%"
:based
git -C "%C%" log -1 --format=commit=%%H >> "%RES%" 2>&1

rem 3. import, then the stages, each in a fresh cmd so the variables the stages set end with them
cd /d "%C%"
set "TMO_STEP_OUT=%C%\godot\.godot"
set "TMO_STEP=ship import"
call "%C%\tools\pc\tmo.bat" %SHIP_IMPORT_CAP% "%GD%" --headless --path "%C%\godot" --import > "%O%\log_import.txt" 2>&1
set EL=%ERRORLEVEL%
echo import exit %EL% >> "%RES%"
if not "%EL%"=="0" set "FAILED=import"
if defined FAILED goto collect
cmd /c ""%~f0" ship"
set EL=%ERRORLEVEL%
echo stages exit %EL% >> "%RES%"
if not "%EL%"=="0" set "FAILED=a stage (see the SKIPPED line above)"
if defined FAILED goto collect
echo %STAGES% | findstr /c:"7_export" > nul
if errorlevel 1 (
  echo checks and shots SKIPPED: STAGES has no 7_export, so nothing was installed >> "%RES%"
  goto collect
)

rem 4. the installed glb must be this run's
set "GLB=%C%\godot\assets\ships\%CODE%\%CODE%.glb"
powershell -NoProfile -Command "$f = Get-Item -LiteralPath $env:GLB -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue; if (-not $f -or $f.LastWriteTimeUtc.Ticks -lt [long]$env:START_TICKS) { exit 1 }"
if errorlevel 1 set "FAILED=install (%CODE%.glb missing or older than this run)"
if defined FAILED goto collect
echo install fresh: %GLB% >> "%RES%"

rem 5. the three checks on the installed glb; every one must pass
set CHECKFAIL=
set "TMO_STEP_OUT=%O%"
set "TMO_STEP=ship GPU readback"
call "%C%\tools\pc\tmo.bat" %SHIP_CHECK_CAP% "%GD%" --path "%C%\godot" --script res://render/test/installed_ship_check.gd -- %CODE% > "%O%\log_readback_gpu.txt" 2>&1
set EL=%ERRORLEVEL%
echo gpu readback exit %EL% >> "%RES%"
if not "%EL%"=="0" set "CHECKFAIL=%CHECKFAIL% readback"
set "TMO_STEP=ship authored interiors"
call "%C%\tools\pc\tmo.bat" %SHIP_CHECK_CAP% "%GD%" --headless --path "%C%\godot" --script res://fx/test/authored_interiors_check.gd -- "%GLB%" > "%O%\log_interiors.txt" 2>&1
set EL=%ERRORLEVEL%
echo authored interiors exit %EL% >> "%RES%"
if not "%EL%"=="0" set "CHECKFAIL=%CHECKFAIL% interiors"
set "TMO_STEP=ship death seam"
call "%C%\tools\pc\tmo.bat" %SHIP_CHECK_CAP% "%GD%" --headless --path "%C%\godot" --script res://fx/test/death_seam_check.gd -- "%GLB%" > "%O%\log_death_seam.txt" 2>&1
set EL=%ERRORLEVEL%
echo death seam exit %EL% >> "%RES%"
if not "%EL%"=="0" set "CHECKFAIL=%CHECKFAIL% death_seam"
if defined CHECKFAIL set "FAILED=checks:%CHECKFAIL%"
set "TMO_STEP=ship shots"
if defined FAILED goto collect

rem 6. the shots, only now: everything above passed, so the installed glb is this run's and checked
call "%C%\tools\pc\tmo.bat" %SHIP_SHOTS_CAP% "%GD%" --path "%C%\godot" --resolution 1920x1080 res://assets/ships/test/a4_shots.tscn -- --out "%O%\shots" --facs %FACS% --sets %SETS% --codes %CODE% > "%O%\log_shots.txt" 2>&1
set EL=%ERRORLEVEL%
echo shots exit %EL% >> "%RES%"
if not "%EL%"=="0" set "FAILED=shots"

rem 7. copy (never move) the recipe folder into out\recipe, so the Board can bring it back (R334)
:collect
if not exist "%V%\recipe\%SHIP%" (
  echo collect: no recipe\%SHIP% folder >> "%RES%"
  if not defined FAILED set "FAILED=collect (no recipe\%SHIP% folder)"
  goto finish
)
xcopy /e /i /y /q "%V%\recipe\%SHIP%" "%O%\recipe" > "%O%\log_collect.txt" 2>&1
set EL=%ERRORLEVEL%
echo collect exit %EL% >> "%RES%"
if not "%EL%"=="0" if not defined FAILED set "FAILED=collect"
echo %STAGES% | findstr /c:"7_export" > nul
if errorlevel 1 goto finish
if not exist "%O%\recipe\export\%CODE%\%CODE%.glb" if not defined FAILED set "FAILED=collect (out\recipe\export\%CODE%\%CODE%.glb missing)"

:finish
rem no parenthesised block here: FAILED can hold a ")" that would end the block early
if defined FAILED goto finish_failed
echo done PASS %DATE% %TIME% >> "%RES%"
echo all done >> "%RES%"
exit /b 0
:finish_failed
echo done FAILED: %FAILED% %DATE% %TIME% >> "%RES%"
echo all done >> "%RES%"
exit /b 1

:ship
rem the stages, in their own cmd (called above as: this file ship); SHIP, C, O, RES and STAGES come from the caller
set RECIPE_NOPAUSE=1
set "INSTALL_TO=%C%\godot"
set CAMJ=
set FAILED=
for %%S in (%STAGES%) do call :stage %%S
if defined FAILED (
  echo checks and shots SKIPPED: stage %FAILED% failed, so the installed glb is not this run's >> "%RES%"
  exit /b 1
)
exit /b 0

:stage
if defined FAILED (
  echo %1 SKIPPED: stage %FAILED% failed >> "%RES%"
  exit /b 0
)
echo %1 start %TIME% >> "%RES%"
call "%C%\tools\recipe\%1.bat" > "%O%\log_%1.txt" 2>&1
set EL=%ERRORLEVEL%
echo %1 exit %EL% at %TIME% >> "%RES%"
if not "%EL%"=="0" set FAILED=%1
exit /b 0

+The PC watchdog caps each solo claim at 1200 seconds by default. A longer bench sets `PC_GPU_SOLO_MAX_S` for that
+step, and a job with several timed sessions claims the card once per session. Each wrapped step has a ceiling sized to
+its own work and an idle check for console output, new files in its output folder, and CPU time across its tree. The
+idle limit is 300 seconds for Godot and 600 seconds for Blender and other tools. A step can raise it with `TMO_IDLE_S`.
+Known fatal lines and 50 consecutive copies of one error line stop the step immediately. A stop returns 124, keeps the
+child logs, and records the step and reason in `timeouts.log`. The watchdog verifies the tree is gone before releasing
+the GPU claim. GPU admission has its own clock, with an 1800-second default in the agent runners and 3600 in the shared
+ship runner. A Jolt stall in the issue 1078 probe held the GPU solo for almost an hour and queued work timed out behind
+it (Board, Nov 19, issue 1192).
+
 3.5 **Every PC queue job is wrapped in the tmo.bat watchdog, ends with exit /b, and carries no |, &, < or > in its
 arguments.** Agent runs stop after STALL_MIN minutes without progress (default 30), or at a hard ceiling (default six
 hours; CODEX_CAP and WORK_CAP override the ceiling in seconds). Progress is a changed file write time in the run repo,

# Line endings in the repo, not a flag in every script (issue 357).
# Git stores LF in the repository and checks out the ending each file type needs.
# .bat and .cmd must be CRLF for cmd; the rest stay LF so a plain clone on the PC reads and runs them.
*.js text eol=lf
*.gd text eol=lf
*.json text eol=lf
*.sh text eol=lf
*.md text eol=lf
*.py text eol=lf
*.patch text eol=lf
*.bat text eol=crlf
*.cmd text eol=crlf

            if [ -n "$ititle" ]; then title="Issue $n: $ititle"; else title=$(printf '%s\n' "$TITLE" | head -1); fi
            if [ -n "$arm" ]; then title="$title [$arm arm]"; fi
            body=$(printf '%s\n' "$TITLE" | tail -n +2)
            printf '%s\n\nOpened automatically from branch %s. The Board reviews and merges.\n' "$body" "$BRANCH" > body.md
            # Claude-built work (opus/) gets an outside check before its issue may close, and standing issues stay open
            # through every merge (256 is the outside-check issue; it was closed by merges twice on Nov 10): those PRs say
            # "Refs #N", never a closing word. Add a standing issue to STANDING (the step env above), space separated.
            word="Closes"
            case "$BRANCH" in opus/*) word="Refs" ;; esac
            [ -z "$n" ] && word="none"
            case " $STANDING " in *" $n "*) word="Refs" ;; esac
            if [ "$word" = "none" ]; then
              : # no issue number in the branch name: the commit text says what it refers to
            elif [ "$word" = "Refs" ]; then
              # any case, with or without a colon: GitHub reads "CLOSES #N" and "Closes: #N" as closing words too
              sed -i -E "s/\b(close[sd]?|fix(e[sd])?|resolve[sd]?):?[[:space:]]+#$n\b/Refs #$n/Ig" body.md
              grep -q "Refs #$n" body.md || printf '\nRefs #%s\n' "$n" >> body.md
            else
              grep -q "Closes #$n" body.md || printf '\nCloses #%s\n' "$n" >> body.md
            fi
            guard_standing
            limit_body
            # If another run opened it in the meantime, that is a pass, not a failure.
            gh pr create -R "$REPO" --base main --head "$BRANCH" --title "$title" --body-file body.md || {
              sleep 3
              if [ "$(gh pr list -R "$REPO" --head "$BRANCH" --state open --json number --jq 'length')" != "0" ]; then
                echo "PR for $BRANCH was opened by another run"
              elif [ "$(gh api "repos/$REPO/compare/main...$BRANCH" --jq .ahead_by 2>/dev/null)" = "0" ]; then
                # Muse creates its branch from main a second before its first commit: nothing to open yet, the next push opens
                # it (Nov 15: each such run failed and mailed Ryan).
                echo "branch $BRANCH has no commits ahead of main yet: no pull request on this push"
              else
                exit 1
              fi
            }
          else
            # A later push from a runner (codex/, muse/, work/) refreshes the text, so the PR describes the newest run
            # (issue 357: PR 543 kept a first run's report after a second run replaced it). opus/ PR bodies are
            # written by hand with their evidence and are never overwritten.
            case "$BRANCH" in
              opus/*) echo "PR already open for $BRANCH" ;;
              *)
                pr=$(gh pr list -R "$REPO" --head "$BRANCH" --state open --json number --jq '.[0].number')
                body=$(printf '%s\n' "$TITLE" | tail -n +2)
                printf '%s\n\nRefreshed from the latest push to branch %s. The Board reviews and merges.\n' "$body" "$BRANCH" > body.md
                # standing issues are never closed by a merge, same as above
                case " $STANDING " in *" $n "*)
                  sed -i -E "s/\b(close[sd]?|fix(e[sd])?|resolve[sd]?):?[[:space:]]+#$n\b/Refs #$n/Ig" body.md
                  grep -q "Refs #$n" body.md || printf '\nRefs #%s\n' "$n" >> body.md ;;
                *) [ -z "$n" ] || grep -qiE "(close[sd]?|fix(e[sd])?|resolve[sd]?|refs):?[[:space:]]+#$n\b" body.md || printf '\nCloses #%s\n' "$n" >> body.md ;;
                esac
                guard_standing
                limit_body
                gh pr edit "$pr" -R "$REPO" --body-file body.md && echo "PR $pr text refreshed"
                ;;
            esac
          fi

@echo off
rem Ship recipe, stage 7: the game export, automated (issue 209 round 7; FINDINGS R155, R156; issue 363).
rem From the baked ship (stage 5) and the ship json, with no hand steps, this writes the game's ship folder into
rem Downloads\voidsworn_test\recipe\SHIP\export\CODE\ : CODE.glb (one ship node with a child node per hard point: hp_w per weapon
rem muzzle, hp_eng, hp_bay, hp_breach, hp_core, hp_rcs, hp_board) and the 2048 map set (export7.py), plus manifest_entry.json
rem with the point counts. Then it reads the glb back and checks it (check_export.py): FAIL when a required point is missing
rem (a weapon slot of the ship's data.js weapon list, an engine, a hangar mouth, the core, the braking and boarding origins), or
rem the breach count for the ship's class and faction does not match the one table (tools/blender/kits/shared/hardpoints.py),
rem or a hard point is off the mesh; see docs/ship_pipeline/HARDPOINTS.md. The standing checks (outline per third, inner edges,
rem second camera, paint sides, blink sheet, licences, light sizes) must also pass before anything is written.
rem Last, it proves the check itself can fail (check_export_selftest.py: every broken copy must be refused, each for its reason).
rem It never copies into the game on its own. To install a checked export, set INSTALL_TO to a Godot project folder (the folder
rem that holds project.godot) before running this. The installer checks a snapshot, locks the project, prepares backups,
rem then installs the checked files and refreshes the ship cache. It checks the installed hashes and reads the ship back
rem as the game draws it. On failure it restores the old files (issues 592, 1101).
rem Set RECIPE_NOPAUSE=1 to run it from another job without the key press at the end.
call "%~dp0env.bat"
set EX=%JOB%\export\%CODE%
rem issue 592: a ship with no paint stack yet ("bake": "none") is exported and checked as an unbaked test export
set UNB=
if /i "%BAKE%"=="none" set UNB=unbaked=1
if not "%STACK_NOTE%"=="" echo Stage 7: %STACK_NOTE%: an unbaked test export
if not exist "%JOB%\%CELL%.blend" (echo Stage 7 FAILED: no baked ship at %JOB%\%CELL%.blend. Run stage 5 first. & goto bad)
if not exist "%EX%" mkdir "%EX%"
echo RUNNING > "%EX%\export_status.txt"
"%BPY%" "%RC%\standing.py" all --ship "%R209_SHIP%" --job "%JOB%" > "%JOB%\log_standing_all.txt" 2>&1
if errorlevel 1 (type "%JOB%\log_standing_all.txt" & echo Stage 7 FAILED: a standing ship check did not pass. See %JOB%\log_standing_all.txt & goto bad)
type "%JOB%\log_standing_all.txt"
setlocal
set "PC_GPU_MODE=none"
call "%REPO%\tools\pc\tmo.bat" 21600 "%B%" -b "%JOB%\%CELL%.blend" -P "%RC%\export7.py" -- "%R209_SHIP%" "%EX%" maps=2048 %UNB% > "%JOB%\log_export.txt" 2>&1
endlocal
findstr /c:"[export7]" "%JOB%\log_export.txt"
findstr /b /c:"WRITTEN" "%EX%\export_status.txt" > nul
if errorlevel 1 (echo Stage 7 FAILED: the export was not written. See %JOB%\log_export.txt & goto bad)
if not "%INSTALL_TO%"=="" goto install
"%BPY%" "%RC%\check_export.py" "%R209_SHIP%" "%EX%" %UNB% > "%JOB%\log_export_check.txt" 2>&1

if not "%CHK%"=="0" (echo CHECK FAILED > "%EX%\export_status.txt" & echo Stage 7 FAILED: %EX% is not a game ship folder. Nothing was installed. & goto bad)

echo CHECKED >> "%EX%\export_status.txt"

- `run_timeout.ps1` and `tmo.bat` now stop idle, fatal, or over-cap steps with exit `124`, retain child output, and verify the whole process tree is gone before releasing its GPU claim.
- The ship and recipe launchers use named step caps. The Codex and work runners default to a 1,800-second GPU admission wait, and the ship runner keeps its 3,600-second default. Updated Rulebook 3.4 and the standing watchdog tests.

+  public long[] ActivePids() {
+    // JOBOBJECT_BASIC_PROCESS_ID_LIST has two DWORD counts followed by pointer-sized PIDs.
+    int capacity = 16;
+    while (true) {
+      int size = 8 + capacity * IntPtr.Size;
+      IntPtr data = Marshal.AllocHGlobal(size);
+      try {
+        if (QueryInformationJobObject(job, 3, data, size, IntPtr.Zero)) {
+          long[] pids = new long[Marshal.ReadInt32(data, 4)];
+          for (int i = 0; i < pids.Length; i++) pids[i] = Marshal.ReadIntPtr(data, 8 + i * IntPtr.Size).ToInt64();
+          return pids;
+        }
+        int error = Marshal.GetLastWin32Error();
+        if (error != 234) throw new Win32Exception(error); // ERROR_MORE_DATA
+        capacity = Math.Max(capacity * 2, Marshal.ReadInt32(data));
+      } finally { Marshal.FreeHGlobal(data); }
+    }
+  }

+function Stop-StepTree($Job, $Log, $Step, $ReportSeconds = 60) {
+  # Keep the owner alive until Windows verifies the tree is gone. The slot helper reclaims dead owners.
+  $clock = [Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
+  $nextReport = 1.0
+  $nextKill = 0.0
+  $killError = ''
+  while ($true) {
+    $usage = $null
+    try { $usage = $Job.ReadUsage() }
+    catch { $killError = $_.Exception.Message }
+    if ($null -ne $usage -and -not $usage.Active) { break }
+
+    $elapsed = $clock.Elapsed.TotalSeconds
+    if ($elapsed -ge $nextReport) {
+      try { $pids = ($Job.ActivePids() | Sort-Object) -join ', ' }
+      catch { $pids = "unavailable ($($_.Exception.Message))" }
+      if (-not $pids) { $pids = 'none in latest snapshot' }
+      $detail = if ($killError) { " ($killError)" } else { '' }
+      Write-KillLog $Log "KILL FAILED $Step; surviving PIDs: $pids. Still retrying with any GPU claim held$detail"
+      $nextReport = $elapsed + $ReportSeconds
+    }
+    if ($elapsed -ge $nextKill) {
+      try { $Job.Kill(); $killError = '' }
+      catch { $killError = $_.Exception.Message }
+      $nextKill = $elapsed + 1
+    }

from pathlib import Path

def replace(file, changes):
    target = Path(file)
    raw = target.read_bytes()
    crlf = b'\\r\\n' in raw
    content = raw.decode().replace('\\r\\n', '\\n')
    for old, new in changes:
        assert content.count(old) == 1, (file, old)
        content = content.replace(old, new, 1)
    target.write_bytes((content.replace('\\n', '\\r\\n') if crlf else content).encode())

+  public long[] ActivePids() {
+    // JOBOBJECT_BASIC_PROCESS_ID_LIST has two DWORD counts followed by pointer-sized PIDs.
+    int capacity = 16;
+    while (true) {
+      int size = 8 + capacity * IntPtr.Size;
+      IntPtr data = Marshal.AllocHGlobal(size);
+      try {
+        if (QueryInformationJobObject(job, 3, data, size, IntPtr.Zero)) {
+          long[] pids = new long[Marshal.ReadInt32(data, 4)];
+          for (int i = 0; i < pids.Length; i++) pids[i] = Marshal.ReadIntPtr(data, 8 + i * IntPtr.Size).ToInt64();
+          return pids;
+        }
+        int error = Marshal.GetLastWin32Error();
+        if (error != 234) throw new Win32Exception(error); // ERROR_MORE_DATA
+        capacity = Math.Max(capacity * 2, Marshal.ReadInt32(data));
+      } finally { Marshal.FreeHGlobal(data); }
+    }
+  }

+function Stop-StepTree($Job, $Log, $Step, $ReportSeconds = 60) {
+  # Keep the owner alive until Windows verifies the tree is gone. The slot helper reclaims dead owners.
+  $clock = [Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
+  $nextReport = 1.0
+  $nextKill = 0.0
+  $killError = ''
+  while ($true) {
+    $usage = $null
+    try { $usage = $Job.ReadUsage() }
+    catch { $killError = $_.Exception.Message }
+    if ($null -ne $usage -and -not $usage.Active) { break }
+
+    $elapsed = $clock.Elapsed.TotalSeconds
+    if ($elapsed -ge $nextReport) {
+      try { $pids = ($Job.ActivePids() | Sort-Object) -join ', ' }
+      catch { $pids = "unavailable ($($_.Exception.Message))" }
+      if (-not $pids) { $pids = 'none in latest snapshot' }
+      $detail = if ($killError) { " ($killError)" } else { '' }
+      Write-KillLog $Log "KILL FAILED $Step; surviving PIDs: $pids. Still retrying with any GPU claim held$detail"
+      $nextReport = $elapsed + $ReportSeconds
+    }
+    if ($elapsed -ge $nextKill) {
+      try { $Job.Kill(); $killError = '' }
+      catch { $killError = $_.Exception.Message }
+      $nextKill = $elapsed + 1
+    }
     Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 50
   }
   Write-KillLog $Log "$Step kill verified: all $($usage.Total) processes gone (root PID $($Job.Root.Id))"

+  public long[] ActivePids() {
+    // JOBOBJECT_BASIC_PROCESS_ID_LIST has two DWORD counts followed by pointer-sized PIDs.
+    int capacity = 16;
+    while (true) {
+      int size = 8 + capacity * IntPtr.Size;
+      IntPtr data = Marshal.AllocHGlobal(size);
+      try {
+        if (QueryInformationJobObject(job, 3, data, size, IntPtr.Zero)) {
+          long[] pids = new long[Marshal.ReadInt32(data, 4)];
+          for (int i = 0; i < pids.Length; i++) pids[i] = Marshal.ReadIntPtr(data, 8 + i * IntPtr.Size).ToInt64();
+          return pids;
+        }
+        int error = Marshal.GetLastWin32Error();
+        if (error != 234) throw new Win32Exception(error); // ERROR_MORE_DATA
+        capacity = Math.Max(capacity * 2, Marshal.ReadInt32(data));
+      } finally { Marshal.FreeHGlobal(data); }
+    }
+  }

+function Stop-StepTree($Job, $Log, $Step, $ReportSeconds = 60) {
+  # Keep the owner alive until Windows verifies the tree is gone. The slot helper reclaims dead owners.
+  $clock = [Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
+  $nextReport = 1.0
+  $nextKill = 0.0
+  $killError = ''
+  while ($true) {
+    $usage = $null
+    try { $usage = $Job.ReadUsage() }
+    catch { $killError = $_.Exception.Message }
+    if ($null -ne $usage -and -not $usage.Active) { break }
+
+    $elapsed = $clock.Elapsed.TotalSeconds
+    if ($elapsed -ge $nextReport) {
+      try { $pids = ($Job.ActivePids() | Sort-Object) -join ', ' }
+      catch { $pids = "unavailable ($($_.Exception.Message))" }
+      if (-not $pids) { $pids = 'none in latest snapshot' }
+      $detail = if ($killError) { " ($killError)" } else { '' }
+      Write-KillLog $Log "KILL FAILED $Step; surviving PIDs: $pids. Still retrying with any GPU claim held$detail"
+      $nextReport = $elapsed + $ReportSeconds
+    }
+    if ($elapsed -ge $nextKill) {
+      try { $Job.Kill(); $killError = '' }
+      catch { $killError = $_.Exception.Message }
+      $nextKill = $elapsed + 1
+    }
     Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 50
   }
   Write-KillLog $Log "$Step kill verified: all $($usage.Total) processes gone (root PID $($Job.Root.Id))"

+  $proxy | Add-Member ScriptMethod Kill {

+  public long[] ActivePids() {
+    // JOBOBJECT_BASIC_PROCESS_ID_LIST has two DWORD counts followed by pointer-sized PIDs.
+    int capacity = 16;
+    while (true) {
+      int size = 8 + capacity * IntPtr.Size;
+      IntPtr data = Marshal.AllocHGlobal(size);
+      try {
+        if (QueryInformationJobObject(job, 3, data, size, IntPtr.Zero)) {
+          long[] pids = new long[Marshal.ReadInt32(data, 4)];
+          for (int i = 0; i < pids.Length; i++) pids[i] = Marshal.ReadIntPtr(data, 8 + i * IntPtr.Size).ToInt64();
+          return pids;
+        }
+        int error = Marshal.GetLastWin32Error();
+        if (error != 234) throw new Win32Exception(error); // ERROR_MORE_DATA
+        capacity = Math.Max(capacity * 2, Marshal.ReadInt32(data));
+      } finally { Marshal.FreeHGlobal(data); }
+    }
+  }

+function Stop-StepTree($Job, $Log, $Step, $ReportSeconds = 60) {
+  # Keep the owner alive until Windows verifies the tree is gone. The slot helper reclaims dead owners.
+  $clock = [Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
+  $nextReport = 1.0
+  $nextKill = 0.0
+  $killError = ''
+  while ($true) {
+    $usage = $null
+    try { $usage = $Job.ReadUsage() }
+    catch { $killError = $_.Exception.Message }
+    if ($null -ne $usage -and -not $usage.Active) { break }
+
+    $elapsed = $clock.Elapsed.TotalSeconds
+    if ($elapsed -ge $nextReport) {
+      try { $pids = ($Job.ActivePids() | Sort-Object) -join ', ' }
+      catch { $pids = "unavailable ($($_.Exception.Message))" }
+      if (-not $pids) { $pids = 'none in latest snapshot' }
+      $detail = if ($killError) { " ($killError)" } else { '' }
+      Write-KillLog $Log "KILL FAILED $Step; surviving PIDs: $pids. Still retrying with any GPU claim held$detail"
+      $nextReport = $elapsed + $ReportSeconds
+    }
+    if ($elapsed -ge $nextKill) {
+      try { $Job.Kill(); $killError = '' }
+      catch { $killError = $_.Exception.Message }
+      $nextKill = $elapsed + 1
+    }
     Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 50
   }
   Write-KillLog $Log "$Step kill verified: all $($usage.Total) processes gone (root PID $($Job.Root.Id))"

+  public long[] ActivePids() {
+    // JOBOBJECT_BASIC_PROCESS_ID_LIST has two DWORD counts followed by pointer-sized PIDs.
+    int capacity = 16;
+    while (true) {
+      int size = 8 + capacity * IntPtr.Size;
+      IntPtr data = Marshal.AllocHGlobal(size);
+      try {
+        if (QueryInformationJobObject(job, 3, data, size, IntPtr.Zero)) {
+          long[] pids = new long[Marshal.ReadInt32(data, 4)];
+          for (int i = 0; i < pids.Length; i++) pids[i] = Marshal.ReadIntPtr(data, 8 + i * IntPtr.Size).ToInt64();
+          return pids;
+        }
+        int error = Marshal.GetLastWin32Error();
+        if (error != 234) throw new Win32Exception(error); // ERROR_MORE_DATA
+        capacity = Math.Max(capacity * 2, Marshal.ReadInt32(data));
+      } finally { Marshal.FreeHGlobal(data); }
+    }
+  }

+function Stop-StepTree($Job, $Log, $Step, $ReportSeconds = 60) {
+  # Keep the owner alive until Windows verifies the tree is gone. The slot helper reclaims dead owners.
+  $clock = [Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
+  $nextReport = 1.0
+  $nextKill = 0.0
+  $killError = ''
+  while ($true) {
+    $usage = $null
+    try { $usage = $Job.ReadUsage() }
+    catch { $killError = $_.Exception.Message }
+    if ($null -ne $usage -and -not $usage.Active) { break }
+
+    $elapsed = $clock.Elapsed.TotalSeconds
+    if ($elapsed -ge $nextReport) {
+      try { $pids = ($Job.ActivePids() | Sort-Object) -join ', ' }
+      catch { $pids = "unavailable ($($_.Exception.Message))" }
+      if (-not $pids) { $pids = 'none in latest snapshot' }
+      $detail = if ($killError) { " ($killError)" } else { '' }
+      Write-KillLog $Log "KILL FAILED $Step; surviving PIDs: $pids. Still retrying with any GPU claim held$detail"
+      $nextReport = $elapsed + $ReportSeconds
+    }
+    if ($elapsed -ge $nextKill) {
+      try { $Job.Kill(); $killError = '' }
+      catch { $killError = $_.Exception.Message }
+      $nextKill = $elapsed + 1
+    }
     Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 50
   }
   Write-KillLog $Log "$Step kill verified: all $($usage.Total) processes gone (root PID $($Job.Root.Id))"

+  public long[] ActivePids() {
+    // JOBOBJECT_BASIC_PROCESS_ID_LIST has two DWORD counts followed by pointer-sized PIDs.
+    int capacity = 16;
+    while (true) {
+      int size = 8 + capacity * IntPtr.Size;
+      IntPtr data = Marshal.AllocHGlobal(size);
+      try {
+        if (QueryInformationJobObject(job, 3, data, size, IntPtr.Zero)) {
+          long[] pids = new long[Marshal.ReadInt32(data, 4)];
+          for (int i = 0; i < pids.Length; i++) pids[i] = Marshal.ReadIntPtr(data, 8 + i * IntPtr.Size).ToInt64();
+          return pids;
+        }
+        int error = Marshal.GetLastWin32Error();
+        if (error != 234) throw new Win32Exception(error); // ERROR_MORE_DATA
+        capacity = Math.Max(capacity * 2, Marshal.ReadInt32(data));
+      } finally { Marshal.FreeHGlobal(data); }
+    }
+  }

-function Stop-StepTree($Job, $Log, $Step) {
-  # Keep the claim while Windows still reports active processes, including during cleanup after a watchdog error.
-  while (($usage = $Job.ReadUsage()).Active) {
-    try { $Job.Kill() }
-    catch { Write-KillLog $Log "$Step kill failed, retrying with GPU claim held: $($_.Exception.Message)" }
+function Stop-StepTree($Job, $Log, $Step, $ReportSeconds = 60) {
+  # Keep the owner alive until Windows verifies the tree is gone. The slot helper reclaims dead owners.
+  $clock = [Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
+  $nextReport = 1.0
+  $nextKill = 0.0
+  $killError = ''
+  while ($true) {
+    $usage = $null
+    try { $usage = $Job.ReadUsage() }
+    catch { $killError = $_.Exception.Message }
+    if ($null -ne $usage -and -not $usage.Active) { break }
+
+    $elapsed = $clock.Elapsed.TotalSeconds
+    if ($elapsed -ge $nextReport) {
+      try { $pids = ($Job.ActivePids() | Sort-Object) -join ', ' }
+      catch { $pids = "unavailable ($($_.Exception.Message))" }

+  public long[] ActivePids() {
+    // JOBOBJECT_BASIC_PROCESS_ID_LIST has two DWORD counts followed by pointer-sized PIDs.
+    int capacity = 16;
+    while (true) {
+      int size = 8 + capacity * IntPtr.Size;
+      IntPtr data = Marshal.AllocHGlobal(size);
+      try {
+        if (QueryInformationJobObject(job, 3, data, size, IntPtr.Zero)) {
+          long[] pids = new long[Marshal.ReadInt32(data, 4)];
+          for (int i = 0; i < pids.Length; i++) pids[i] = Marshal.ReadIntPtr(data, 8 + i * IntPtr.Size).ToInt64();
+          return pids;
+        }
+        int error = Marshal.GetLastWin32Error();
+        if (error != 234) throw new Win32Exception(error); // ERROR_MORE_DATA
+        capacity = Math.Max(capacity * 2, Marshal.ReadInt32(data));
+      } finally { Marshal.FreeHGlobal(data); }
+    }
+  }
   public void Kill() {

+function Stop-StepTree($Job, $Log, $Step, $ReportSeconds = 60) {
+  # Keep the owner alive until Windows verifies the tree is gone. The slot helper reclaims dead owners.
+  $clock = [Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
+  $nextReport = 1.0
+  $nextKill = 0.0
+  $killError = ''
+  while ($true) {
+    $usage = $null
+    try { $usage = $Job.ReadUsage() }
+    catch { $killError = $_.Exception.Message }
+    if ($null -ne $usage -and -not $usage.Active) { break }
+
+    $elapsed = $clock.Elapsed.TotalSeconds
+    if ($elapsed -ge $nextReport) {
+      try { $pids = ($Job.ActivePids() | Sort-Object) -join ', ' }
+      catch { $pids = "unavailable ($($_.Exception.Message))" }
+      if (-not $pids) { $pids = 'none in latest snapshot' }
+      $detail = if ($killError) { " ($killError)" } else { '' }
+      Write-KillLog $Log "KILL FAILED $Step; surviving PIDs: $pids. Still retrying with any GPU claim held$detail"
+      $nextReport = $elapsed + $ReportSeconds
+    }
+    if ($elapsed -ge $nextKill) {
+      try { $Job.Kill(); $killError = '' }
+      catch { $killError = $_.Exception.Message }
+      $nextKill = $elapsed + 1
+    }

   }

// Issue 946: under --fast a row runs when it is marked fast or it is the gate.
function fastSelected(checks) {
  return checks.filter(c => c.fast || c.gate);
}

// ---------- running ----------

function printRow(row) {
  console.log(row.result + ': ' + row.check + ' (' + row.seconds.toFixed(2) + ' s) | ' + row.detail);
  if (row.result === 'FAIL') console.log('\nFAIL ' + row.check + ' (first 15 output lines):\n' + row.output.split(/\r?\n/).slice(0, 15).join('\n'));
}

"""Fault checks for stage 7. All writes go to a temporary project."""
import json
from pathlib import Path
import struct
import tempfile
from unittest.mock import patch

import manifest_merge as stage


CODE = 'TEST_1101'
GLB = CODE + '.glb'
JPG = CODE + '_base.jpg'
NORMAL = CODE + '_normal.png'
ORM = CODE + '_parts_orm.png'
OLD_MAP = CODE + '_old.jpg'
OLD_PNG = CODE + '_old_normal.png'


def write_glb(path, *maps):
    doc = json.dumps({'images': [{'uri': name} for name in maps]}).encode('utf-8')
    doc += b' ' * (-len(doc) % 4)
    glb = struct.pack('<4sIII4s', b'glTF', 2, 20 + len(doc), len(doc), b'JSON') + doc
    path.write_bytes(glb)


def make_export(export):
    export.mkdir(parents=True)
    write_glb(export / GLB, JPG, NORMAL, ORM)
    (export / JPG).write_bytes(b'checked texture')
    (export / NORMAL).write_bytes(b'checked normal')
    (export / ORM).write_bytes(b'checked orm')
    (export / OLD_MAP).write_bytes(b'left over from a previous export')
    (export / stage.ENTRY).write_text('{"hardpoints":{"core":1}}', encoding='utf-8')


def check_stray_maps(root):
    export, snapshot = root / 'export', root / 'checked'
    make_export(export)
    snapshot.mkdir()
    for stray in (CODE + '_base.png', '../' + NORMAL, 'OTHER_normal.png'):
        write_glb(export / GLB, JPG, NORMAL, ORM, stray)
        try:
            stage.export_snapshot(export, snapshot, CODE)
        except ValueError as exc:
            assert 'injected' in str(exc) or 'differs from the checked export' in str(exc), exc
        else:
            assert fault is None, f'{fault} was accepted'

    if fault:
        assert all(path.read_bytes() == data for path, data in originals.items()), 'the previous install changed'
        assert refreshed == (fault in ('refresh', 'readback', 'changed_installed_map'))

        assert (backup / CODE / GLB).read_bytes() == originals[live / GLB]
        assert (backup / 'asset_manifest.json').read_bytes() == originals[manifest]
        assert (backup / 'ship.scn').read_bytes() == originals[scene]
    # The lock also releases on a failed transaction.
    with stage.project_lock(project):
        pass
    print(f'PASS {fault or "install"}')
